# Worklog

---
## Task ID: 2-a
**Agent:** Explore-frontend
**Task summary:** Read-only deep trace of the FUSIONONE frontend (`/home/z/my-project/frontend`) covering the invoice domain, invoice rendering/components, @react-pdf/renderer usage, @napi-rs/canvas PDF→PNG pipeline, WhatsApp domain + API routes, Supabase platform layer, app routes/auth, env/config (Caddyfile, ENVIRONMENT_SETUP.md), and shared/sales/proformas/parties touchpoints — to feed a refactor plan that (a) swaps React-PDF→PDFKit on the frontend, (b) moves PDF/image generation to the backend with WhatsApp sends carrying only {invoiceId}, (c) removes the multi-template system, (d) removes the PDF→image pipeline.

**Work Log:**
- Read `frontend/package.json` — confirmed deps: `@react-pdf/renderer ^4.5.1`, `@napi-rs/canvas ^0.1.68`, `@supabase/ssr`, `@supabase/supabase-js`, `next ^16.2.7`, no axios (native fetch only).
- Read every file in `domains/invoice/`: `types.ts` (InvoiceData/InvoiceStore/InvoiceParty/InvoiceLineItem/InvoiceTradeIn/TemplateVariant), `registry.ts` (6-template registry), `builders.ts` (sale/purchase/proforma → InvoiceData), `view-model.ts` (formatCurrency/amountInWords/InvoiceViewModel), `delivery.ts` (resolveDeliveryMessage placeholder renderer), `actions/client.ts` (browser embedImages + POST /api/invoice + /api/invoice/png + triggerDownload), `renderers/pdf.ts` (renderToStream server-side), `renderers/png/{export,layout,assets,utilities}.ts` (@napi-rs/canvas Prestige-only PNG drawer).
- Read all 6 templates (`Prestige/Classic/Minimal/Retail/Executive/Heritage.tsx`) — confirmed each duplicates fmt + numberToWords + icons + StyleSheet; only colors/fonts/layout differ; all single `<Page size="A4">`, no pagination/wrap.
- Read `components/invoice/` (InvoiceReview, InvoiceSidebar, InvoiceWhatsAppShare, index.ts) — traced WhatsApp send trigger incl. sessionStorage auto-send key `fusion-one.whatsapp-auto-send` set by sales/new, purchases/new, proformas/new.
- Read `domains/whatsapp/` (types.ts, client.ts, service.ts, index.ts) and `domains/delivery/` (types.ts, backend-service.ts, index.ts) — documented WHATSAPP_BACKEND_URL/WHATSAPP_BACKEND_API_TOKEN, POST /api/whatsapp/sendInvoice body {recipient, image(dataURL), caption, requestId}, SSE /api/events bridging.
- Read all `app/api/**` routes: invoice (PDF), invoice/png, invoice/delivery, whatsapp (GET/POST/DELETE), whatsapp/events (SSE), whatsapp/qr, whatsapp/diagnostics, accounts (add-funds/transactions/transfer).
- Read `platform/supabase/{client,server,admin}.ts` + `platform/index.ts` + `platform/services/accounts.ts` — mapped browser/server/admin clients and env vars (NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL, NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY; admin used ONLY server-side in platform/services/accounts.ts).
- Read `proxy.ts` (Next 16 middleware-replacement: session refresh + auth redirects; matcher excludes /api), `next.config.ts` (standalone output; serverExternalPackages: @napi-rs/canvas, @react-pdf/renderer), both Caddyfiles (:81 → localhost:3000 default, XTransformPort query override), ENVIRONMENT_SETUP.md, PRODUCTION_READINESS.md (stale — references removed Puppeteer/wa-engine), APPLICATION_CLEANUP_REPORT.md.
- Read app routes: (app) layout + sales/[id], sales/[id]/edit, sales/new, sales list, purchases/[id], purchases/new, proformas/[id], proformas/new, settings (template picker + WhatsApp delivery panel), exchange, payments, dashboard, accounts, inventory, financial-year, parties; (auth) login/onboarding; app/actions/auth.ts.
- Read sales/purchases mutations (bill numbering SAL-/PUR-/PUR-TRD-/PI- + financial_years counters, trade-in auto-purchases, payments_in, account_transactions, cancel logic), proformas queries, parties queries, shared types/hooks/providers.
- Cross-checked SUPABASE_BOOTSTRAP.sql (20 tables incl. store.invoice_templates JSONB default, whatsapp_settings) and sql/ migrations incl. void_migration.sql (superseded header: "Cancel Migration (replaces void_migration.sql)").
- Verified no `.env.local` in frontend; repo root `.env` only has a legacy Prisma DATABASE_URL; WHATSAPP_BACKEND_* vars exist only as code references in domains/whatsapp/client.ts.

**Stage Summary (key findings):**
- **InvoiceData model** (`domains/invoice/types.ts`): {type, template, store, bill_number, date, party, items[], subtotal, item_discount?, additional_discount?, discount?, trade_in_credit?, final_total, paid, due, trade_ins?}. Builders map Supabase rows (sales/sale_items/trade_ins/store/parties; purchases/purchase_items; proforma_invoices/proforma_invoice_items/proforma_trade_ins) into it entirely client-side.
- **PDF flow (to keep locally, swap to PDFKit):** browser `downloadInvoicePdf` → embedImages (logo/signature URL→base64 JPEG via canvas) → POST `/api/invoice` → server `renderPdf()` (`@react-pdf/renderer` renderToStream) → registry picks 1 of 6 templates → binary PDF → browser blob download. Filename `${bill_number}.pdf`.
- **Template system (to remove):** 6 variants (prestige/classic/minimal/retail/executive/heritage); selected per invoice type in Settings → persisted in `store.invoice_templates` JSONB; sales pages honor it, but purchase/proforma detail pages HARDCODE `template:'prestige'`; PNG pipeline ignores template entirely (always Prestige-style); every template duplicates fmt/numberToWords/icons (~2,250 LOC total, only Prestige is effectively canonical).
- **PDF→image pipeline (to remove):** server-only `@napi-rs/canvas` drawer `drawPrestigeInvoice` (1600px wide, dynamic height, Arial, PNG buffer) invoked by `/api/invoice/png` (download) and `/api/invoice/delivery` (WhatsApp attachment). NOT a PDF rasterization — an independent canvas re-implementation of the Prestige design.
- **WhatsApp send (to reduce to {invoiceId}):** browser `InvoiceWhatsAppShare.send()` → POST `/api/invoice/delivery` {invoice: FULL InvoiceData, to, caption} → server renders PNG → `deliveryService.deliver()` → `whatsappService.sendMessage()` → base64 data URL → POST `${WHATSAPP_BACKEND_URL}/api/whatsapp/sendInvoice` with Bearer `WHATSAPP_BACKEND_API_TOKEN`, body {recipient, image, caption, requestId}. Backend never touches Supabase and never receives any invoice id/UUID — only pre-rendered pixels + resolved caption text.
- **Data backend cannot currently get:** nothing structurally missing from Supabase (all invoice rows, store row incl. invoice_templates, whatsapp_settings templates, parties.number are in DB), BUT the backend has no Supabase client and no invoice-lookup code; today it receives only: resolved caption text, recipient digits, and a base64 PNG. Auto-send intent lives only in browser sessionStorage (`fusion-one.whatsapp-auto-send` = `type:billNumber`). Item-level discounts, amount-in-words, and message placeholder resolution are computed client-side from DB values.
- **Delivery status:** SSE only (backend /api/events → Next `/api/whatsapp/events` → browser EventSource); no polling; events state-changed/qr-available/qr-countdown/send-result/security-event.
- **Supabase auth:** cookie-based Supabase Auth via @supabase/ssr; proxy.ts refreshes session and guards pages (api routes excluded from matcher; invoice/whatsapp routes have NO auth of their own — local-app security model); backend receives only a shared Bearer token, no user identity.
- **Dead/duplicate code:** 5 non-canonical templates (Classic/Minimal/Retail/Executive/Heritage) + their registry entries; numberToWords duplicated 8× (6 templates + view-model + InvoiceReview); fmt duplicated similarly; cancel logic duplicated between `sales/[id]/page.tsx` handleCancel and `domains/sales/mutations.ts` cancelSale; `sql/void_migration.sql` header says it is the replacement cancel migration (legacy void status gone); PRODUCTION_READINESS.md describes removed Puppeteer engine (stale doc); root `.env` DATABASE_URL is a Prisma leftover.
- **Env matrix:** NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL/ANON_KEY (browser client + server client + proxy), SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY (server-only admin client, accounts service), WHATSAPP_BACKEND_URL + WHATSAPP_BACKEND_API_TOKEN (server-only whatsapp client). No .env.example; ENVIRONMENT_SETUP.md documents only the 3 Supabase vars (WHATSAPP_BACKEND_* undocumented there). Caddy: port 81 reverse proxy to :3000 (or XTransformPort query override) — single-origin assumption, no separate WhatsApp backend route.

- Phase G: frontend PDFKit renderer port + server-side RLS-scoped repository + /api/invoice rewrite (authenticated, Content-Disposition attachment) + id-based client download action; pdfkit installed, @react-pdf/renderer + @napi-rs/canvas removed.
- (Template system removal executed within F/G due to compile coupling — all six templates, registry, renderers, PNG route, useStoreTemplates, Settings picker; verified via E2E before deletion via preserved golden artifacts.)
- Phase H: formal visual reconciliation — final PDF set through the frontend renderer for all 5 representative cases; structural line measurements match (±2.2pt); pixel-diff 4.88–8.58% (intentional: logo/signature/page-numbers/minus); VLM verdicts: MATERIAL PARITY (sale, trade-in, proforma).

- Phase J: sql/drop_invoice_templates.sql migration created (idempotent + verification); DDL not possible via REST keys (verified no exec path) → documented as one-time owner dashboard action; SUPABASE_BOOTSTRAP.sql aligned.
- Phase K: backend 264/264 tests + typecheck + tsc build clean; frontend tsc + eslint clean; live E2E (login, PDF download 74,785 B, purchase+proforma PDFs, WhatsApp send → expected NOT_CONNECTED toast, auto-send intent flow, settings, 11 pages, mobile, zero console errors); restart simulation (node_modules wiped) → full self-heal; REFACTOR_IMPLEMENTATION.md written (15 sections).

Stage Summary:

Task ID: 3 (preview-URL fix — main app unreachable through the platform preview proxy)
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: Owner reported the main app unreachable at the preview URL while /backend worked. Directive: remove the backend from the proxy, serve only the main app, and do not fight the platform ("the impossible").

Work Log:
- Diagnosed through the live preview host: the platform's external preview edge intercepts the EXACT path `/dashboard` (301 → `/dashboard/`, HTML body + Refresh header, before the sandbox Caddy). Next.js trailing-slash:false normalization then 308s `/dashboard/` → `/dashboard` → infinite redirect loop → browser error. Proven: /dashboard 301→/dashboard/ (edge), /dashboard/ 308→/dashboard (Next), XTransformPort bypass attempts also intercepted; every OTHER app path (/login, /sales, /settings, ...) passes through untouched. Not fixable at Caddy (immutable, root-owned) — platform behavior is a hard constraint.
- Removed the `/backend/:path*` → `localhost:3001` rewrite from frontend/next.config.ts per owner directive (preview now serves ONLY the main app; /backend/* → 404). Verified the app never depended on it — all backend calls are server-to-server via WHATSAPP_BACKEND_URL (domains/whatsapp/client.ts); the backend process keeps running on :3001 as an internal dependency.
- Renamed the main route `app/(app)/dashboard` → `app/(app)/home` (path /home verified un-intercepted: clean 404 passthrough before the change, /login-/sales-style passthrough after). Updated every URL reference: app/page.tsx (root redirect), proxy.ts (isAppRoute guard + authed redirect target), Sidebar nav href (label stays "Dashboard"), login + onboarding router.push, proformas/new + sales/new closed-FY fallbacks. domains/dashboard module and React-Query ['dashboard'] cache keys intentionally unchanged (not URLs). Explanatory comments added at each site.
- Updated reference docs: workspace Caddyfile header + DEV_CREDENTIALS.md §4 (routing + /home note).
- Restarted frontend cleanly via .zscripts/dev.sh (idempotent, backend untouched on :3001).
- Full E2E through the REAL preview host with agent-browser: `/` → 307 `/home` → login guard `/login?redirect=/home` → sign-in (owner account) → `/home` renders (FY 2026–2027, IN STOCK 14 live units); sidebar Sales → list (SAL-0001..0004) → detail SAL-2026-27-0003; PDF golden path POST /api/invoice {invoiceId, invoiceType} → 200 application/pdf `attachment; filename="SAL-2026-27-0003.pdf"`; WhatsApp send → POST /api/whatsapp/send-invoice 400 = expected safe WHATSAPP_NOT_CONNECTED (proves internal :3001 backend call path intact without the proxy rewrite); Dashboard sidebar link → /home; mobile 390×844 no horizontal overflow; 0 console errors, 0 page errors. Screenshots: pdf-template-ref/preview-{home-fixed,home-desktop,home-mobile}.png.
- ESLint clean, `tsc --noEmit` clean; dev.log shows the full verified request chain.
- Committed to sandbox root git: a35757b.

Stage Summary:
- Preview URL now serves ONLY the main app and it WORKS end-to-end (was: infinite redirect loop on /dashboard).
- /backend/* no longer reachable through the preview (404) per owner directive; backend process kept running internally on :3001 — required by the app's WhatsApp features, called server-to-server, unaffected by the proxy removal.
- Main page canonical URL is now /home (label still "Dashboard"); /dashboard must never be reused as an app route on this platform.
- Platform constraint documented in 3 places (proxy.ts, next.config.ts, Caddyfile + DEV_CREDENTIALS.md) so future work doesn't regress it.

Task ID: 4 (port swap — backend live at preview root; E2E WhatsApp invoice sends; realtime status bug fix)
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: Owner directives: (1) swap frontend/backend so the BACKEND is live at the preview URL, (2) wait ~3 min for owner to login via QR (do not end chat), (3) test the backend end-to-end by sending a few invoices to the owner's number 8795103722 (temporarily switching party numbers; no spam), (4) fix the "status not updating in realtime" bug in the frontend WITHOUT introducing websocket.

Work Log:
- SWAP: backend/.env PORT 3001→3000; frontend/.env.local WHATSAPP_BACKEND_URL→http://localhost:3000; .zscripts/dev.sh ports swapped; frontend/package.json dev script -p 3000→3001 (it hardcoded the port — first restart attempt raced the backend and lost: backend EADDRINUSE-crashed; fixed by the package.json edit + clean restart).
- Backend dashboard: backend/client (Vite reference client) changed to same-origin by default (VITE_BACKEND_URL ?? '' — was hardcoded localhost:3000 fallback), VITE_API_TOKEN baked at build (dev sandbox only, served through session-scoped preview origin); `bun run build` → client/dist; backend serves it at / via new @fastify/static registration in src/api/server.ts (GET / → index.html, falls back to JSON info if unbundled).
- Owner logged in on the LIVE dashboard: clicked Login ~20:23:10 (QR + countdown events flowing), scanned, backend CONNECTED at ~20:27 with jid 918795103722:45@s.whatsapp.net (the owner's number 8795103722).
- E2E SEND TESTS (3 invoices, all three types; party numbers temporarily PATCHed in Supabase to 8795103722, restored + verified immediately after):
  1. SAL-2026-27-0003 (sale, MOHD KAIF) via backend API direct → 200 {success, messageId 3EB03D529B9DA5DE61A804}, PDF 74,539 B.
  2. PUR-2026-27-0005 (purchase, MOHD TARIQ KHAN) via backend API direct → 200 {messageId 3EB02AEB1033CBC0D8DDA2}, PDF 74,476 B.
  3. PI-2026-27-0001 (proforma, WHO) via FRONTEND UI click (Send via WhatsApp) → POST /api/whatsapp/send-invoice 200 → {messageId 3EB00BF444C8C8BE2F3C3B}, PDF 74,680 B.
  All 3 send-result events verified; party numbers restored (8/8 verified against pre-test snapshot).
- REALTIME BUG — root causes found & fixed (SSE only, no websocket):
  a) domains/whatsapp/service.ts: when the backend SSE stream died (restart/drop), startEventStream left sseConnection set to the dead object → ensureEventStream early-returned forever → service permanently deaf, cache frozen. FIX: finally{} always clears the connection + scheduleSseReconnect() with capped exponential backoff (1s→30s), backoff reset on any received envelope, refreshStatus resync on every reconnect (SSE has no replay).
  b) hooks/useWhatsAppPlatform.ts mapBackendState: only understood UPPERCASE backend states; the bridge's initial event and the service's synthetic subscribe event carry lowercase business states ('connected') → fell to default 'disconnected' → panel froze on "disconnected" even while connected. FIX: accept both shapes (PAIRING/pairing, CONNECTED/connected, ...).
  c) app/api/whatsapp/route.ts GET + qr/route.ts returned the service cache with no refresh (and route.ts had a confused no-op refreshInBackground). FIX: await whatsappService.refreshStatus() (now on the WhatsAppService interface) before responding.
  d) app/api/whatsapp/events/route.ts: initial event used the possibly-stale cache. FIX: await refreshStatus() before the initial send.
  e) domains/whatsapp/client.ts fetchStatus: added AbortSignal.timeout(8000) so a hanging backend can't block route handlers (long-lived SSE fetch untouched).
  f) refreshStatus gained in-flight deduplication (concurrent callers share one fetch).
- VERIFICATION of the fix: in-browser EventSource test listener captured the full live chain in real time — initial fresh snapshot (connected + jid), synthetic subscribe event, and send-result {"requestId":"e2e-test-sale-001","recipient":"918795103722@s.whatsapp.net","result":"success"} arriving at the exact ms of the backend send. Settings → WhatsApp Delivery panel now shows "connected / 918795103722:45@s.whatsapp.net" (screenshot pdf-template-ref/whatsapp-panel-connected.png). Backend restart-reconnect path covered by the service reconnect logic (typechecked; live stream health proven by the send-result delivery).
- Observed (platform, not fixable): the external preview edge drops idle-ish SSE connections ~every 25s; browsers' EventSource auto-reconnect handles it (user's dashboard QR flow worked fine through the churn).
- Typecheck/lint: backend tsc clean; frontend tsc + eslint clean. Committed to sandbox root git.

Stage Summary:
- BACKEND IS LIVE at the preview root (dashboard + API on :3000); frontend internal on :3001; owner's WhatsApp CONNECTED (jid 918795103722:45@s.whatsapp.net).
- Full E2E proven for all three invoice types: {invoiceId, invoiceType} → Supabase load → PDFKit PDF → Baileys document+caption message → delivered to the owner's phone (3 messages, no spam).
- Realtime status bug FIXED (2 real bugs: service never reconnected dead SSE; hook mis-mapped business-state payloads) + 3 hardening fixes (fresh status on GET routes/SSE initial, fetch timeout, refresh dedupe). No websocket introduced — pure SSE.
- Party numbers temporarily switched for testing and fully restored (verified 8/8). No business data permanently modified.

Task ID: 5
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: Owner reported production build 404s on every CSS/JS/logo asset. Directives: (1) fix the production build asset loading, (2) swap the backend out of the preview root and put the PRODUCTION frontend there instead, started via `npm start`, (3) remove the standalone build configuration.

But there WAS a real code gap for the preview: the installed Baileys (6.7.18) only auto-generates jpegThumbnail for IMAGE and VIDEO messages (prepareWAMessageMedia: requiresThumbnailComputation = mediaType==='image'||'video') — never documents. Manual attaches get their first-page preview because the WhatsApp app rasterizes page 1 client-side and embeds it. Our sends had no thumbnail → bare file tile in chats.
- FIX: new backend/src/invoice/thumbnail.ts — generatePdfFirstPageThumbnail(pdf) rasterizes PDF page 1 (~300px wide JPEG q0.78) and it is embedded as documentMessage.jpegThumbnail (extra content keys are spread into the message by Baileys — verified in source).
- Rasterizer journey (documented for future maintainers):
  1. In-process @napi-rs/canvas under Bun: HARD SEGFAULT of the bun runtime (bun 1.3.14, crash report 1.3.14/Ba10d9b296…) — unacceptable in the backend process → moved rasterization to a short-lived plain-NODE child process (stdin: PDF bytes → stdout: JPEG; node is the production runtime anyway). Crash-isolated: any child failure → warning + null → send proceeds without preview. 10s hard timeout.
  2. @napi-rs/canvas 1.0.9 under node: "double free or corruption" native abort during render → pinned to 0.1.68 (the version the old frontend PNG pipeline used in this sandbox).
  3. pdfjs-dist 3.11.174 requires the unbuilt node-canvas package (dead end) → stayed on pdfjs-dist 4.10.38 (its NodeCanvasFactory requires @napi-rs/canvas directly — no node-canvas needed). Removed the stray unbuilt node_modules/canvas left by the v3 experiment.
- Deps added (backend): pdfjs-dist@4.10.38 (exact), @napi-rs/canvas@0.1.68 (exact). Both pinned — the combo is version-fragile (see journey above).
- Wiring: invoice/send.ts step 4.5 generates the thumbnail after the PDF render; SendInput gained optional `thumbnail` (logged as thumbnailBytes); SendController passes it through; WhatsAppManager.sendDocumentMessage gained optional 5th param → sets jpegThumbnail when non-empty.
- Verification: worker tested on all 6 golden invoice PDFs × 2 runs — 12/12 deterministic byte-stable JPEGs (~2.3KB each); VLM visual check confirms readable "TAX INVOICE" text + rendered logo + proper invoice layout (standard-font data loading works via pdfjs-dist/standard_fonts).

Full-path trace, fix root causes only, no polling, no backend rewrite, offline tests, live E2E verification, SSE_QR_FIX_REPORT.md (English).

Work Log:
- INVESTIGATION (read-only, complete path traced): backend verified CORRECT — WhatsAppManager emits WHATSAPP_QR_AVAILABLE per Baileys QR with generation-scoped countdown (qrGenerationId guard), event bus validates closed schema, SSE endpoint broadcasts data:{type,timestamp,data} with 30s keepalive; production Next.js frontend (next start, :3000) with whatsappService singleton (one backend SSE connection — confirmed via backend SSE client logs: 1 client per backend boot). All defects in the Next.js layer.
- ROOT CAUSES (each reproduced with a FAILING deterministic test before the fix): RC1 hook's initial REST fetch had a generation guard that discarded the snapshot whenever ANY SSE event arrived during the fetch — qr-countdown ticks every 1s during pairing so the QR-bearing snapshot was dropped essentially always on page/tab open; RC2 the bridge's initial state-changed carries the full status (incl. qrCode) but the hook's handler ignored QR fields; RC3 no stale-QR protection (delayed older-generation qr-available/countdown could overwrite newer QR); RC4 QR never cleared when leaving pairing; RC5 all four handlers duplicated in the manual-reconnect path; RC6 manual reconnect permanently gave up after 10 attempts; RC7 service subscribe() emitted a synthetic {state}-only event (inconsistent shape) and forwarded SERVER_STATE_CHANGED as state-changed {state:'running'} → spurious disconnect flash; RC8 service REST refresh could overwrite newer event-applied state + nulled countdown on unchanged QR; RC9 (found in live E2E) service reconnect-refresh updated the cache but never notified browsers (UI stale after backend-stream drop).
- FIX (frontend only; backend ZERO changes): NEW domains/whatsapp/state.ts — the ONE pure event→status normalizer (state mapping both forms, QR freshness rule: live events require monotonic expiresAt, snapshots authoritative-current accepted / provably-older rejected, QR cleared when leaving pairing, stale countdown rejection, no-op identity for React bailout); hook rewritten thin (NO initial REST fetch — bridge's first SSE event IS the snapshot so the RC1 race class cannot exist; ONE attach() for initial+reconnect; functional updates; retry forever at the 30s cap); service transitions through the same normalizer, SERVER_STATE_CHANGED internal-only, appliedEventSeq race guard, countdown preserved on unchanged QR, NEW applySnapshotAndNotify (refresh that CHANGES the cache emits a full-status state-changed to subscribers = reconnect recovery; silent when unchanged; no announce on first cold snapshot); bridge gap-free (subscribe→buffer→refresh→initial→flush); panel pairing-without-QR intermediate branch.
- LIVE BUG FOUND & FIXED DURING E2E: the real backend REST snapshot carries the QR WITHOUT expiresAt — my initial strict freshness rule rejected it (browser showed "QR AVAILABLE: NO" with a live backend QR). Fixed with the snapshot-vs-live distinction (snapshotQrAllowed) + 2 regression tests.
- TESTS: frontend vitest infra added (vitest+jsdom+@testing-library, dev-only); 44/44 deterministic tests (21 normalizer, 12 hook w/ MockEventSource incl. manual-reconnect lifecycle, 11 service w/ mocked client incl. race guard + recovery notification); tsc clean; eslint clean; next build clean (all 27 routes) and deployed to :3000. Backend untouched: 283/283 green.
- LIVE E2E (agent-browser): REAL backend — settings reflects real state; login → real Baileys QR instantly with live countdown; NINE successive real QR generations replaced in place across continuous observation (hashes verified different, countdowns cycling); QR expiry text then next generation; tab switch away/back → QR reappears INSTANTLY (the original bug scenario); Next-server restart under the browser → state recovered with NO reload. ISOLATED mock backend (:3099) + second prod instance (:3100, env-overridden WHATSAPP_BACKEND_URL): full lifecycle connect→QR#1→QR#2→backend-restart→7s recovery→connect→scan→connected→logout→disconnected+QR cleared→connect again; mock held exactly 1 SSE client (single service connection). A login was initiated from the UI during verification, leaving the system PAIRING with a live QR for the owner to re-scan (one scan reconnects the phone).

Stage Summary:
- Client architecture: browser singleton (RLS) does ~90% of work incl. ALL multi-step mutations; server cookie client only for auth checks + invoice PDF repository; admin (RLS-bypassing) ONLY in platform/services/accounts.ts (add-funds/transfer/transactions API).
- Data fetching is SPLIT-BRAIN: list pages use TanStack useQuery (staleTime 5min, gcTime 30min, retry 1, no refetch on focus/reconnect) but ALL 4 detail pages (sales/[id], sales/[id]/edit, purchases/[id], proformas/[id]) use raw useEffect+useState with NO cache; FinancialYearProvider, Sidebar, financial-year page, settings WhatsAppDeliveryPanel also raw-fetch. Mutations are plain async fns (no useMutation) with hand-rolled, inconsistent invalidation lists (8 keys in sales/new vs 4 in sales/page vs 5 in edit).
- DUPLICATION (18 cases documented, key ones): bank_accounts+payment_modes fetched under 4 different query keys (sales-page/purchases-page/form-dropdown-data/accounts-page) + dashboard bank_accounts = 5 fetch paths for the same dropdown data; sales & purchases FY rows fetched 3× each (list page, parties ledger, dashboard) with different column sets; account_transactions (bank_account_id,type,amount) fetched identically by accounts page, dashboard, and financial-year close; store table fetched at 8 distinct sites (3 raw useEffects run on EVERY app mount: FinancialYearProvider, Sidebar, financial-year page); sale detail (sale+sale_items+trade_ins+store) implemented 4× (fetchSaleDetail, sales/[id] loadSale, sales/[id]/edit useEffect, server repository); cancel-sale logic fully duplicated (domains/sales/mutations.cancelSale vs sales/[id]/page.tsx handleCancel inline); accounts page inlines bank/payment-mode CRUD that duplicates the (dead) domains/accounts/mutations.ts; settings WhatsAppDeliveryPanel raw-fetches whatsapp_settings that useWhatsAppDeliverySettings already caches.
- OVERFETCH/inefficiency: .select('*') on sales, purchases, proforma_invoices, parties, trade_ins, inventory_items, store, purchase_items, sale-detail sale_items; dashboard + parties pages pull ALL FY rows to aggregate client-side (should be SQL/RPC aggregates/GROUP BY); NO pagination/limit on any list page; N+1 sequential awaits in createSale trade-in loop, cancelSale loops, sales/[id]/edit per-item updates; proformas/[id] fetches trade_ins sequentially after a Promise.all; FY bill counters read-then-update race (4 sites) with no atomic increment — no RPC exists to fix any of this (zero .rpc usage).
- Realtime: none via Supabase; only custom SSE bridge (/api/whatsapp/events → backend /api/events) with backoff reconnect, subscribe/unsubscribe in route handler; DB freshness relies solely on manual query invalidation.
- RLS: all 20 business tables RLS-enabled; is_owner() SECURITY DEFINER; anon denied by default; storage buckets public-read with authenticated-only writes, no per-user object isolation (documented as accepted single-tenant trade-off).

- FIXED (state.ts): applyQrCountdown rejects countdowns in known non-pairing states (pristine default exempt — preserves existing mid-pairing-connect test semantics).
- FIXED (useWhatsAppPlatform.ts): healthyTimer cleared on unmount/stream end; finished stream reference nulled.
- Tests: NEW src/test/whatsappSse.test.ts (10 transport tests), +2 state tests, +2 hook tests, +1 backend test → 66/66 frontend, 288/288 backend, tsc clean both sides.
- LIVE VERIFICATION (all through the Vite app against the hosted backend): logout → UI disconnected realtime; Connect → pairing + QR + countdown (49s→41s at ~1/s); QR rotation ≥3 generations at ~60s cadence over the 3-min wait; USER SCANNED THE QR FROM THE FRONTEND (~18:30) → UI transitioned to connected with jid 918795103722:54@s.whatsapp.net in realtime, page alive 5.7min continuously, ZERO new SSE connections across the whole lifecycle (one stream, no reload, no polling); offline drill post-fix → no wedge, UI correct, zero errors; backend later dropped to LOGGED_OUT externally (user unlinked or Render restart) → frontend showed correct state via remount snapshot (parity).

Agent: main (GLM 5.3 direct implementation)
Task: FUSIONONE — invoice list action cleanup: remove per-row preview icons, keep whole-row detail navigation, make three-dot menu actions (Save PDF / Share / Print) execute directly without navigating through the detail page, preserve PAY and detail-page actions, verify live.

Work Log:
- Audited every list for the pattern: Sales/Purchases/Proformas lists carry the FileText preview icon + ⋮ menus whose items navigate to detail (Purchases/Proformas all three items are navigate stubs; Sales Share/Print navigate); Party detail + Parties lists are pure navigation rows (already compliant); Dashboard/Payments/Inventory/Accounts/Exchange have no invoice-row action pattern (Exchange's FileText is an external document link — out of scope).
- Shared layer: download.ts refactored to export buildInvoicePdf (the single compose step: load by reference → builders → browser PDFKit) with downloadInvoicePdf behavior unchanged; new print.ts (printInvoicePdf = same buildInvoicePdf + transient 1px iframe handing the blob to the browser's native print UI, cleanup on completion); new useInvoiceQuickActions.ts hook — one behavior/toast layer (savePdf/share/print) for all lists; Share reuses the existing WhatsApp delivery utility postSendInvoice by reference (no second implementation).
- Page fixes: removed the FileText/View button from all 3 lists (no replacement icon); replaced the navigate-stub handlers with direct quickActions calls; SalesPage dead code removed (menu-open detail prefetch + loadingMenuId spinner — obsolete once actions execute directly; cancel dialog fetches on demand, now with try/catch + error toast instead of unhandled rejection); ⋮ trigger/Receive/Pay stopPropagation guards unchanged.
- ROOT CAUSE found by live event tracing (agent-browser capture-phase listeners): the ⋮ action menus are createPortal(document.body) but nested in the row's REACT subtree — React synthetic events bubble through the React tree, so every menu-item click ALSO fired the row's onClick → navigate(detail). This made ALL menu actions "behave like navigation" even where handlers were direct (pre-existing bug, invisible before because the stubs navigated anyway). Fixed with proper event handling: the portal menu root now stops propagation (onClick stopPropagation) — one guard per menu covering every item; verified via instrumented event log: menu clicks dispatch action only, row clicks navigate only.
- Verification (agent-browser, desktop 1440 + mobile 390, dev :3000): Sales — 0 preview icons on 8 rows; row click → /sales/:id; ⋮ menu opens with URL stays; Save PDF stays on /sales with pipeline queries fired from the list; Print stays (0 stray iframes, adapter lifecycle clean); Share POSTs /api/whatsapp/sendInvoice directly (backend PAIRING/not connected → 503 → clean error toast; no real message sent); Receive modal opens without navigation; detail page action system intact. Purchases — Pay → Record Payment modal (no navigation), Save PDF + Print direct, row → detail. Proformas — same matrix green. Party detail — tab rows navigate to detail pages. Mobile 390px — [Receive][⋮] layout intact, menu fits viewport (right edge 349px), not clipped. Zero console/page errors across the session.
- Gates: tsc --noEmit 0; vitest 91/91; bun run build OK; dead-reference sweep clean (no title="View", no list FileText, no loadingMenuId, no navigate-stub handlers); single PDF compose path confirmed (only download.ts composes; print.ts reuses).
- Wrote INVOICE_LIST_ACTION_CLEANUP.md.

Stage Summary:
- Final interaction model live everywhere: CLICK ROW → detail; CLICK PAY/Receive → payment modal only; CLICK ⋮ → menu only; CLICK SAVE PDF/SHARE/PRINT → direct execution, never list → detail → action.
- One implementation per action: buildInvoicePdf (download + print share it), postSendInvoice (detail + list share it), one quick-actions hook for all lists; zero duplicated PDF rendering, zero duplicated share logic, no new dependencies.
- The React-portal bubbling bug (menu clicks leaking into row navigation) is fixed at its root with a single stopPropagation guard per menu — the exact pre-existing defect the task described as "menu actions behave like navigation".
- Known pre-existing gap (documented, untouched): bun run lint has no ESLint config in the Vite tree (config only ever existed under old/nextjs) — broken since the Vite migration, unrelated to this change.
- Files: src/features/invoice/{download.ts (refactor), print.ts (new), useInvoiceQuickActions.ts (new)}, src/pages/{sales/SalesPage,purchases/PurchasesPage,proformas/ProformasPage}.tsx, INVOICE_LIST_ACTION_CLEANUP.md (new). Screenshots: tool-results/invoice-cleanup/.

Task ID: frontend-performance-audit
Agent: main (GLM 5.3 direct implementation)
Task: FUSIONONE — frontend performance + loading-path audit and correction: measure the real Invoice Detail → cached-PDF path, fix only demonstrable bottlenecks, preserve the existing architecture (one PDF pipeline, one query cache, one renderer).

Work Log:
- Audited the full invoice path end-to-end at code level: useInvoicePdf → buildInvoicePdf → loadInvoiceData → builders → pdf-cache (memory+IndexedDB) → PDFKit renderer → InvoicePdfViewer (pdf.js lazy singleton, progressive pages); detail pages' React Query layer; QueryProvider (staleTime 5 min); invalidate.ts module-scope client; party-detail infinite query; AppShell scroll restoration; lists' keys and skeletons.
- Added dev-gated instrumentation (src/platform/perf.ts + marks in ViewButton/detail pages/useInvoicePdf/download/pdf-cache/viewer). Verified inert in production builds (import.meta.env.DEV dead-code elimination; window.__invoicePerf absent in prod).
- Measured BASELINE in a live browser (cloud Supabase): first open ~2400 ms; second open (mem cache) 950 ms with 742 ms of it re-fetching data; browser refresh (IDB) 1548 ms with 1337 ms re-fetching; plus a spurious second pipeline run per stale mount.
- Root cause: loadInvoiceData bypassed the React Query cache and issued its own Supabase queries (incl. a sequential store→sale waterfall) on every compose — duplicated with the page's query, on every open.
- Fix 1: loadInvoiceData now resolves via queryClient.fetchQuery with the SAME keys/fetchers as the detail pages (getQueryClient() exported from invalidate.ts); fresh option (staleTime 0) for manual refresh; friendly errors kept with cause chaining. Fingerprint equality verified live (old-path cache entries still hit after the change — zero regeneration).
- Fix 2: buildInvoicePdf in-flight dedupe (per type:id:intent) so concurrent composes share one run; manual refresh passes fresh=true.
- Fix 3 (regression I introduced with fix 1, caught by counters): the forced run's own fetch bumped dataUpdatedAt and the self-induced revision bump superseded it (regenerated PDF cached but not displayed). Fixed with a narrow supersede rule in useInvoicePdf (non-forced runs never supersede an in-flight forced run). Verified: refresh = exactly 1 run, 1 generation, new PDF displayed.
- Fix 4: pdf.js idle prewarm (warmInvoicePdfViewer exported from the viewer, AppShell requestIdleCallback) — first invoice open of a session now finds pdf.js warm (201 ms → 5-9 ms measured); pdf.js stays a lazy chunk (build manifest verified).
- AFTER measurements: first open 1271 ms (1 fetch batch = 4 queries, was 8 in 2 batches); second open 214/196 ms with ZERO network; browser refresh 917-981 ms (single batch + IDB hit, 0 generation); manual refresh 1 fresh batch + 1 generation + displayed; party-detail next batch = one 283 ms lean ranged request appending 7 rows (Sunita Devi, 17 invoices).
- Verified in the PRODUCTION build (vite preview on :4173): session, sale/purchase/proforma viewers render (VLM-verified screenshots), cached open = one data batch + zero generation (IndexedDB createdAt proof), re-open = zero network, no instrumentation present.
- Regression sweep: party detail tabs (?tab= restored on Back), list back-navigation, Save PDF list action (IDB hit, 0 generation), dashboard/payments/accounts/inventory/settings clean; no console errors on fresh loads (one HMR artifact during editing, not a code bug).
- tsc --noEmit clean; tsc -b && vite build succeeds (pdf.js 437 KB / PDFKit 537 KB remain separate lazy chunks); bun run lint is broken REPOSITORY-WIDE (no eslint.config exists — pre-existing, out of scope, documented).
- Wrote FRONTEND_PERFORMANCE_AUDIT.md (timing breakdowns, root causes, cache/fingerprint verification, changes, before/after, build results).

Stage Summary:
- Cached invoice opens: 950 ms → 214 ms (0 network on re-open); browser refresh: 1548 ms → ~950 ms; first open: ~2400 ms → ~1270 ms; network per open halved (8 queries/2 sequential batches → 4 queries/1 batch); manual refresh fixed to actually display the regenerated document.
- Architecture preserved: ONE PDF pipeline (buildInvoicePdf), ONE renderer (PDFKit), ONE cache (pdf-cache memory+IndexedDB), ONE data layer (React Query) — no new frameworks, no prefetch-all system, no PDF pregeneration.
- Key files: src/platform/perf.ts (new), src/features/invoice/{api,download,useInvoicePdf,pdf-cache}.ts, src/components/invoice/InvoicePdfViewer.tsx, src/components/AppShell.tsx, src/components/ui/ViewButton.tsx, src/features/invalidate.ts, 3 detail pages (dev marks only), FRONTEND_PERFORMANCE_AUDIT.md.

Task ID: wa-lifecycle-redesign (design phase)
Agent: main (GLM 5.3 direct implementation)
Task: FUSIONONE — WhatsApp Runtime + Session Lifecycle Redesign (spec: specs/implementation-spec.md, 2500 lines, read in full). Redis test credentials provided by user; connectivity PROBED OK (SET/GET/DEL round-trip) via redis:// URL form; node-redis redis@6.3.0 installed in backend/.

Work Log:
- Read the complete spec (all 2500 lines, sections 0–53) before any implementation.
- Forensic inspection of backend: app.ts, index.ts, config, state/{state-machine,whatsapp-states,transitions}, session/{SessionManager,lifecycle}, whatsapp/WhatsAppManager (full 1235 lines), security/SecurityManager, send/{SendController,schema}, invoice/send, watchdog/Watchdog, api/{server,auth,sse}, events/{emitter,registry}.
- Forensic inspection of frontend: platform/whatsapp/{types,state,backend,http,sse,url}, features/whatsapp/useWhatsAppPlatform, components/settings/WhatsAppPlatformPanel, components/invoice/InvoiceWhatsAppShare.
- Confirmed: Redis ABSENT everywhere (no package/code/config). Baileys started ONLY via POST /api/whatsapp/login → startLogin(); single socket owner (WhatsAppManager); NO idle mechanism; transient close → RECONNECTING (uncapped, bounded backoff); intentional close already removes listeners BEFORE socket.end() (no stale close events); shutdown preserves session; logout/security destroy via SessionManager.destroySession (fail-closed); /ping already meets the §36 contract (token, timingSafeEqual, 204/401, zero side effects) — DO NOT rebuild; /health/live + /health/ready purposeful.
- Confirmed §38 defect: SendController.sendWithTimeout creates a setTimeout that is NEVER cleared on success — timer stays alive the full sendTimeoutMs (30s).
- Key architectural flaw per spec §7: LOGGED_OUT conflates "runtime off" with "no session" — the state model has no session dimension.

Stage Summary — TARGET ARCHITECTURE (spec §42 answers):
1. Baileys runtime owner: WhatsAppManager (unchanged; sole socket factory/import).
2. Session lifecycle owner: SessionManager (authoritative destruction + session dimension), with ONE backup module src/session/backup/ (SessionBackup: serialize/encrypt/save/load/invalidate; debounced scheduler) — no Redis calls scattered elsewhere.
3. Idle timer owner: WhatsAppManager via new IdleTimer component (src/whatsapp/IdleTimer.ts); activity = explicit login, wake, connection open, send attempts. NOT: ping/health/SSE/status/watchdog/QR rotation/creds.update/Baileys keepalive.
4. Wake decision: WhatsAppManager.ensureReadyForSend() (single-flight beginStart) for operations; frontend panel auto-wake uses the EXISTING POST /api/whatsapp/login when status = IDLE + session PRESENT.
5. Reconnect allowed: only transient codes from authenticated runtime states; intentional stops remove listeners pre-end so close events never reach the handler; IDLE is not a reconnect-valid state.
6. Primary session persistence: Baileys useMultiFileAuthState on local FS (unchanged).
7. Redis backup manager: SessionBackup (AES-256-GCM at rest, key from WHATSAPP_BACKUP_ENCRYPTION_KEY via scrypt; versioned JSON envelope; whitelisted auth files only); saves debounced from creds.update + on connection open; Redis NEVER consulted when local session is healthy.
8. Stale Redis invalidation: restore attempt → Baileys auth failure (security code from CONNECTING) → existing handleSecurityFailure → destroySession('security') now also invalidates the Redis backup → IDLE + NONE in one pass, no loop.
9. /api/status: whatsapp gains `session: NONE|PRESENT|RESTORING`; runtime states gain IDLE (replaces LOGGED_OUT).
10. SSE: WHATSAPP_STATE_CHANGED payload gains optional `session` field; all other events unchanged; no polling.
11. Connect with no session: unchanged (login → PAIRING → QR).
12. Auto wake: panel sees idle+present → POST login (existing mechanism) → CONNECTING → CONNECTED, no QR; once-per-mount guard prevents loops.
13. sendInvoice while idle: SendController awaits ensureReadyForSend() before assertSendAllowed; no session → WHATSAPP_NOT_CONNECTED (existing UX); concurrent wake converges on one socket/shared readiness.
14. logout/security vs idle: destructive paths destroy local + Redis (fail-closed, SECURITY_SESSION_CLEANUP_FAILED if either cannot be confirmed); idle only stops the runtime, preserving all session material.
- New state set (still 9): STARTING, IDLE, PAIRING, CONNECTING, CONNECTED, RECONNECTING, LOGGING_OUT, SECURITY_INVALIDATED, STOPPING. New transitions incl. CONNECTED→IDLE (idle), PAIRING→IDLE (abandoned pairing), CONNECTING→IDLE, RECONNECTING→IDLE, IDLE→{PAIRING,CONNECTING,LOGGING_OUT}, SECURITY_INVALIDATED→IDLE, LOGGING_OUT→IDLE.
- Config additions: WHATSAPP_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS (default 900000), WHATSAPP_WAKE_TIMEOUT_MS (default 20000), REDIS_URL (default '' = backup disabled), WHATSAPP_BACKUP_ENCRYPTION_KEY (required iff REDIS_URL set). Credentials live ONLY in backend/.env (sandbox-local, already the established convention for secrets) — never in source, .env.example, frontend, logs, or reports.
- /ping, /health/*, QR flow (60s Baileys rotation + backend countdown), invoice pipeline (JWT/RLS/repository/PDF/thumbnail/templates/retry), SSE observer semantics: all preserved.
- §38 fix: sendWithTimeout clears its timer in finally.
- Render persistence (§19): documented as ops requirement (WATSAPP_AUTH_DIR on persistent disk); code logs resolved path at socket creation (already present).

Task ID: redis-sse-qr-fix
Agent: main (GLM 5.3 direct implementation)
Task: FUSIONONE — Redis production connectivity + WhatsApp QR/SSE reactivity fix (targeted, no unrelated work): fix the production "Redis backup client prewarmed → Socket timeout (Expecting data, but didn't receive any in 10000ms)" pattern and the user-reported "QR not replacing / 60s event-based countdown not showing / QR not changing at countdown 0" symptoms.

Work Log:
- INVESTIGATION (read-only first): read SessionBackup.ts, config, WhatsAppManager (QR emission + countdown engine + lifecycle), api/server.ts, api/sse.ts, events/registry.ts, app.ts; frontend state.ts normalizer, sse.ts transport, useWhatsAppPlatform hook, WhatsAppPlatformPanel, backend.ts types, http.ts; both test suites.
- REDIS ROOT CAUSE (reproduced locally in .zscripts/backend.log): node-redis v6 maps socket.socketTimeout to net.Socket.setTimeout() — an IDLE-SOCKET killer that destroys the connection N ms after the last byte in EITHER direction. The backup layer set socketTimeout: 10000, so every healthy connection (prewarm, post-save) was killed exactly 10.002s after the last operation — matching the user's production log pattern precisely (verified: "prewarmed 19:16:07 → error 19:16:17.343"; "saved 20:20:16 → error 20:20:26"). connectTimeout (5s) is the correct establishment bound; per-command bounds were ALREADY enforced by the module's withTimeout() wrapper.
- REDIS FIX (backend/src/session/backup/SessionBackup.ts): removed socketTimeout from createClient (kept connectTimeout 5s, reconnectStrategy:false, per-op withTimeout 10s — no protection removed; the misapplied idle-kill was the bug); documented the node-redis socket-semantics trap in the constants block; withTimeout now tears the client down on the TIMEOUT path only (unknown connection state → destroy → next operation lazily reconnects = recovery on next operation; fast server-side error replies leave the healthy connection intact); added clientCreationCount test seam (idle-survival regression observable).
- SSE/QR EVIDENCE GATHERING: locally reproduced the FULL user paths — long-lived panel (logout → Connect → QR live + countdown ticking + rotation at 60s cadence) and fresh mount during active pairing (QR + countdown immediate, rotation live) — ALL WORKING in current source. Probed PRODUCTION (wa.one.fusiongadgets.in with TEST-project JWT, which it accepts): backend runs the new lifecycle code (IDLE + session field), SSE transport through Cloudflare is fully functional with a browser User-Agent (68s continuous capture: state-changed + qr-available + 67 countdown ticks + 2 rotations, zero silence); earlier "silent death" captures were Cloudflare bot-treating curl's UA (python-urllib got 403 outright). CONCLUSION: current source is correct; the user's production symptoms come from the deployed frontend build being stale PLUS two real hardening gaps (below), now fixed.
- SSE/QR FIX 1 — lifecycle-complete REST snapshot (backend + frontend contract completion): WhatsAppManager now tracks currentQrExpiresAt (set with each QR generation, cleared on connection open + clearQr) and exposes getQrExpiresAt()/getQrExpiresInSeconds(); GET /api/status returns whatsapp.qrExpiresInSeconds + qrExpiresAt; frontend BackendStatusResponse type + applyAuthoritativeSnapshot pass them through. Effect: a fresh mount during pairing renders QR + countdown in the FIRST frame (verified live at t=834ms after reload) and reconnect re-snapshots no longer wipe a known countdown (the state.ts applier already supported the fields — the backend simply never sent them).
- SSE/QR FIX 2 — adaptive stall watchdog: sse.ts gains ConnectAuthenticatedEventStreamOptions.getStallTimeoutMs (re-read on every watchdog re-arm) + exported SSE_PAIRING_STALL_TIMEOUT_MS = 10s; the hook passes () => statusRef.current?.state === 'pairing' ? 10_000 : undefined. Rationale: during PAIRING the backend guarantees 1 event/second (countdown), so 10s of silence = 10 missed ticks = provably-dead stream → recovery in 10s instead of 45s (silent stream deaths were observed on the production path for bot-classified clients; the sandbox preview edge also drops streams ~every 25s). Non-pairing states keep the 45s bound (> the 30s keepalive ping — no false positives).
- TESTS: backend 315→324 (+9: mocked-redis client contract ×5 — no socketTimeout ever configured, open-client reuse, timeout→teardown→reconnect-on-next-op, fast-error no-teardown, save round-trip without URL/plaintext leakage; QR lifecycle accessors ×3; /api/status QR fields ×1) + integration 6→7 (NEW idle-survival regression: save → 12s idle → same connection answers with clientCreationCount===1 — fails on the old code). Frontend 94→101 (+7: snapshot-with-countdown ×2, adaptive stall ×3 incl. re-arm tightening, hook snapshot-first-frame + adaptive wiring ×2; sse mock now forwards arguments, re-exports the constant).
- DOCS: API.md /api/status response + field table extended with qrExpiresInSeconds/qrExpiresAt.
- VERIFICATION: backend 324 + 7/7 integration vs the REAL Redis instance (incl. the 14s idle-survival test); frontend 101; tsc clean both; production builds clean both; clean backend restart with the fix → "Redis backup client prewarmed" + 45s+ idle → ZERO socket-timeout errors (old code errored at exactly +10.002s); live login exercised backupExists() through the warm fixed connection (no error); LIVE QR E2E post-fix: fresh reload during pairing → QR + countdown at t=834ms (snapshot-carried), countdown ticking every second, QR rotation at 21:03:04.606 → panel src changed WITHOUT refresh, countdown restarted at 60→45s; zero browser console errors; evidence screenshot tool-results/redis-sse-fix/final-pairing-live.png.
- DEPLOYMENT NOTE for the owner: production needs BOTH sides redeployed — backend (Render): the Redis fix + status QR fields; frontend (Cloudflare Pages): the current source (the deployed build predates the realtime fixes; verified the production backend + transport are already correct).

Stage Summary:
- Redis: the idle-socket killer (socketTimeout misuse) is eliminated at the root; connection survives idle periods (protocol-level regression test), commands stay bounded (5s connect / 10s per-op), recovery on next operation is guaranteed (timeout → teardown → lazy reconnect), credentials/URLs never logged (test-pinned). Local session wake path never touches Redis; recovery when local is missing remains fully functional (integration-verified against the configured instance).
- SSE/QR: /api/status now carries the full QR lifecycle (countdown lands in the first frame on mount/reconnect — no more wipe/flicker), and a silently-dead pairing stream is detected in 10s instead of 45s. Verified live: Connect → PAIRING → QR immediate; countdown ticking; rotation replaces the QR instantly without refresh; no polling, no duplicate connections, no remounts — the existing /api/status + SSE architecture is preserved exactly.
- All gates: backend 324 unit + 7 integration, frontend 101, tsc ×2, builds ×2, live E2E ×2 (Redis idle survival + reactive QR flow), zero console errors.

Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: FUSIONONE backend WhatsApp runtime/session architecture refactor (per downloaded spec) — Phase 1 INSPECT + Phase 2 DESIGN MAP

Work Log:
- Downloaded and read the full 1500-line implementation specification (live-forensic lifecycle refactor: client presence, demand-driven runtime, 5-minute grace, Redis candidate resolution, generation protection)
- Inspected ALL backend source: WhatsAppManager.ts (1698 lines), IdleTimer.ts, SessionManager.ts, SessionBackup.ts, lifecycle.ts, state machine (whatsapp-states/transitions/state-machine), Watchdog, SecurityManager, SendController, server.ts, sse.ts, auth.ts, app.ts, config, events, errors
- Inspected frontend contract: platform/whatsapp/{sse,backend,http,state}.ts, useWhatsAppPlatform.ts, WhatsAppPlatformPanel.tsx — SSE IS authenticated (fetch + Bearer JWT), frontend is observer-only EXCEPT the panel's auto-wake effect (postLogin on IDLE+PRESENT)
- Inspected all backend tests (idle-wake, lifecycle, whatsapp, session, api, config, session-backup, security, events, state, send, send-pipeline, document-transport, thumbnail)
- Baseline verification: typecheck PASS, full suite 331 passed / 7 skipped (pre-existing Redis-gated skips)

DESIGN MAP (Phase 2):
- Runtime owner: WhatsAppManager (KEEP — one socket, single-flight beginStart, reconnect, QR)
- Session owner: SessionManager (KEEP — NONE/PRESENT/RESTORING, destroySession = the ONE destruction op)
- Redis owner: SessionBackup (KEEP — AES-256-GCM envelope, debounced scheduler, backupGeneration)
- Lifecycle coordinator: LifecycleManager — ADDS sessionGeneration counter (bumped first by every destructive op; captured+checked by async startup/restore before committing)
- Client presence: NEW small ClientPresence class (counter + 0→1 / 1→0 callbacks), fed by the authenticated /api/events SSE route (server.ts wiring), consumed by WhatsAppManager
- Demand model (replaces 15-min IdleTimer — file DELETED): demand = clients present || activeStart in flight || active send op || RECONNECTING/reconnect timer armed. Last client gone + no demand → 5-minute grace (WHATSAPP_CLIENT_DISCONNECT_GRACE_MS, default 300000) → intentional stop (session preserved; abandoned unvalidated pairing residue discarded)
- Auto-wake: first client appears → cancel grace + beginStart('wake') (NEVER pairs); frontend panel auto-wake effect REMOVED (required frontend change — it uses login intent which can pair, violating "client presence never pairs")
- Candidate resolution (NEW): connectCandidate local|redis + lastStartIntent tracked in WhatsAppManager. QR-during-CONNECTING and security-coded-during-CONNECTING = candidate rejection: local rejected + Redis backup exists → try Redis candidate (restore → validate); all candidates exhausted → destroySession (deterministic both-stale cleanup) then PAIRING only if intent=login, else IDLE+NONE
- attemptBackupRestore: typed outcome restored|invalid|unavailable — invalidates the backup ONLY on BackupInvalidError (undecryptable/corrupt/format); Redis timeout/unavailable NEVER invalidates
- Config: remove WHATSAPP_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS, add WHATSAPP_CLIENT_DISCONNECT_GRACE_MS (default 300000)
- Docs: backend/API.md lifecycle sections rewritten to final model; root WHATSAPP_RUNTIME_LIFECYCLE_REDESIGN.md stale 15-minute statements corrected

Stage Summary:
- Inspection + design complete; implementation begins with backend core (config, lifecycle, SessionBackup, ClientPresence, WhatsAppManager), then API wiring, then tests (rewrite idle-wake → runtime-demand suite, new candidate-resolution suite, update lifecycle/config/api suites), then the required frontend panel change, then validation (typecheck/tests/build) and doc updates

- FRONTEND 2: WhatsAppPairingDialog.tsx — reuses ui/Modal; open = state === 'pairing'; QR + countdown + instructions inside dialog; X/Close/Cancel = POST cancelPairing (cancelling state, error keeps dialog open); auto-closes on any state change away from pairing; QR refresh updates in place; Refresh QR moved into the dialog.
- FRONTEND 3: Panel consumes the context; QR block completely removed; CONTENT_MIN_H shrunk to the remaining tallest state; pairing state = compact status + pointer to the pairing window.
- TESTS: backend pairing-cancel.test.ts (new), session-backup.test.ts (retry contract updates), runtime-demand additions (SAVE-failure isolation); frontend whatsappPanel.test.tsx (context mock + QR-removal), whatsappPairingDialog.test.tsx (new), appPresence.test.tsx (new: one SSE per app client, survives route changes, non-blocking render).

Task ID: set-password-isolation-1
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: Fix the real authentication-flow bug: opening a Supabase invitation link lands on /set-password but shows "This link is invalid..." with console 400 "Invalid Refresh Token: Refresh Token Not Found". Trace the exact failure, identify the root cause, implement the correct architectural fix (auth-isolated /set-password), and verify with a real browser harness. Spec doc (downloaded, read fully) = master specification; English-only deliverables.

Work Log:
- Downloaded + read the FULL master spec (4,722 lines) from the user-provided link; key sections for this task: §13 (set-password page), §35 (set-password session isolation), §44 (route structure), §12 (access-state model), §36 (cache/session transition).
- PHASE 1 FORENSIC AUDIT — traced the complete chain: src/platform/supabase/client.ts (createBrowserClient) → @supabase/ssr 0.12.7 createBrowserClient.js (hard-codes flowType:"pkce" AFTER the options spread — NOT overridable) → @supabase/auth-js 2.117.2 GoTrueClient._initialize → _getSessionFromURL (throws 'Not a valid PKCE flow url.' for implicit callbacks when flowType==='pkce') → SessionProvider getSession/__loadSession (refreshes expired stored session) → _callRefreshToken 400 path → _removeSession/SIGNED_OUT → SetPasswordPage phase conflation + premature history.replaceState URL wipe.
- PHASE 2 LIVE REPRODUCTION (agent-browser, real TEST project egdrnhtmclvhsfjvhyam, real admin generateLink invitations):
  * CASE A (fresh browser + invite link): verify 303 → /set-password#access_token=...&type=invite (implicit grant, JWT amr=[otp]) → page showed "This link is invalid, has expired, or has already been used." — NO session adopted (network: zero token calls), URL tokens wiped by the page. REPRODUCED.
  * CASE C (crafted stale normal-session cookie + fresh invite link): same invalid page + console "AuthApiError: Refresh token is not valid" x5 + POST /token?grant_type=refresh_token → 400. REPRODUCED exactly as reported.
  * CASE E (re-click consumed link): GoTrue redirects to /set-password#error=access_denied&error_code=otp_expired&error_description=Email+link+is+invalid+or+has+expired — the ONLY genuine invalid-link evidence, which the old page ignored entirely.
- ROOT CAUSE (proven): (1) @supabase/ssr's createBrowserClient hard-codes PKCE; backend-invited users (admin.inviteUserByEmail, no browser PKCE challenge) get an IMPLICIT hash grant that the client categorically refuses — the invitation session is dropped at init. (2) SetPasswordPage mapped every non-SETUP_CONTEXT state (incl. UNAUTHENTICATED left by the stale-session 400 cleanup) to "invalid link", conflating an unrelated normal-session refresh failure with link validity. (3) The page wiped the URL (history.replaceState) right after the first getSession — destroying the still-valid invitation tokens. (4) SETUP_CONTEXT detection (user.amr === 'otp' only) was fragile (stored user may lack amr after reload).
- PHASE 3 IMPLEMENTATION:
  * NEW src/platform/supabase/setup-flow.ts — single source of truth for the email-link setup flow: readSetupLinkFromLocation (hash+search params: error / implicit / pkce), invalidReasonFromError (otp_expired→EXPIRED, already→ALREADY_USED, else INVALID), sessionAmrMethod (user.amr with access-token JWT fallback — survives reloads), isSetupSession (fail-closed: ONLY 'password' logins are normal app sessions), adoptImplicitSetupSession (native supabase.auth.setSession on the SAME shared client — no second client, no custom tokens).
  * src/components/providers/SessionProvider.tsx — toRawSession now takes the full Session and classifies amr via setup-flow (reload-safe); isSetupContext = raw && amrMethod !== null && amrMethod !== 'password' (documented fail-closed rule; password-login behavior identical to before).
  * src/pages/auth/SetPasswordPage.tsx — rewritten as a self-contained auth-isolated flow with precise internal states: CHECKING / SETUP_CONTEXT_ACTIVE / SETUP_CONTEXT_MISSING / SETUP_CONTEXT_INVALID{EXPIRED|ALREADY_USED|INVALID} / SETUP_SESSION_ERROR{ADOPT_FAILED|CODE_UNUSABLE} and PASSWORD_UPDATE_PENDING/ERROR/SUCCESS. One-shot link processing (StrictMode-safe ref; async continuation not cancelled); URL captured synchronously then cleaned immediately; adoption via setSession; PKCE code path settles the client's own exchange; live onAuthStateChange listener keeps the phase current (never upgrades terminal states). Submit: updateUser → signOut → /login (setup context never becomes an app session).
- VERIFICATION (all live, agent-browser on the preview gateway origin, real Supabase):
  * T1 CASE A: fresh browser + invite link → password FORM renders; URL cleaned. PASS
  * T6: mid-setup navigation to /home → bounced back to /set-password (SETUP_CONTEXT guard). PASS
  * T7: /set-password network log — ZERO PostgREST/business/SSE/WhatsApp requests (no normal access resolution, no onboarding trigger). PASS
  * T8: full page reload of /set-password with adopted setup session → form persists (JWT amr fallback). PASS
  * T11: submit → password set → session terminated (0 auth cookies) → /login. PASS
  * T12: login with the NEW password (amr=password → normal resolution); NULL-role user → /no-access terminal. PASS
  * Full journey: backend-style provisioning (user_type='user') → login → /home full application. PASS
  * T2 CASE B: valid normal session + fresh invite link → form (adoption replaces session). PASS
  * T3 CASE C (THE REPORTED BUG): stale normal refresh token + fresh invite link → FORM RENDERS; the 400 "Refresh token is not valid" console noise still appears (Supabase's own stale-session cleanup) but no longer breaks the flow. PASS
  * T5 CASE E: consumed link → precise "This link has expired." state from error_code=otp_expired. PASS
  * T4 recovery: recovery link (implicit, amr=otp) → form → new password → old password REJECTED, new password → /home. PASS
  * ?code= without verifier (cross-browser PKCE) → CODE_UNUSABLE message ("same browser where the reset was requested"). PASS
  * Direct visit (no session) → SETUP_CONTEXT_MISSING copy. PASS
  * Unverified login → /verify-email; NULL login → /no-access; verified user login → /home. PASS (all states distinct, no regressions)
  * No refresh loop: 35s observation under CASE C conditions — exactly ONE stale-cleanup 400, then stable (library cooldown + adoption). PASS
  * No infinite auth redirects; no duplicate listeners (single subscribe/unsubscribe per component); zero runtime errors in dev.log; vitest 139/139 PASS; tsc --noEmit PASS.
- Cleanup: deleted the 7 e2e-*/invite-probe* test auth users created during reproduction (live project tidy); no forensic instrumentation was added to app code (probes were external scripts only).

Stage Summary:
- Root cause: @supabase/ssr's createBrowserClient hard-codes PKCE flow type, so Supabase invitation emails (backend-invited, implicit hash grants) are categorically refused by the client's detectSessionInUrl; the page then conflated the resulting UNAUTHENTICATED state (incl. stale-session refresh-400 noise) with "invalid link" and wiped the URL tokens.
- Fix: /set-password is now an explicit auth-isolated flow (src/platform/supabase/setup-flow.ts) that adopts implicit grants via the native auth.setSession on the shared client, classifies setup sessions fail-closed by amr (only 'password' is a normal app session — reload-safe via JWT claim), maps GoTrue error params to precise invalid states, and never lets normal-session noise masquerade as link validity.
- Invariant held (live-proven): a valid invitation/recovery link works regardless of stale OR valid normal sessions in the browser; the setup session never enters the application; successful setup always terminates to /login.
- Noted: owner@fusionone.test currently has user_type='user' in the live TEST project (the real owner is wamiq.khan2023@gmail.com, active today) — owner-role READY path is code-identical for password logins (verified via the 'user' READY path + unchanged owner/store code); OWNER_SETUP_REQUIRED branch untouched and previously matrix-verified.
- Known non-code items: `bun run lint` has never worked in this repo (no eslint.config file exists — pre-existing); vite HMR websocket warning through the preview gateway is environmental.

- ROOT-CAUSE ANALYSIS (from pdfkit source + AFM metrics): (1) PDFKit anchors the first line's baseline at y + ascender·size/1000; for Helvetica/Helvetica-Bold ascender(718) == capHeight(718), so the caps/digit box spans [y, y+0.718·size] — the old fixed offsets (10.5/8.5) pinned the box TOP, leaving the text mass ~2pt high (measured on raster: label −2.16pt, amount −1.68pt vs bar midline; gaps 9.6/13.9 and 8.2/11.5). (2) fit:[75,30] + align:center centers the image within a 75pt-wide box anchored at the block's LEFT edge → image center lands 33.75pt left of the block center (measured: 456.9pt vs 491.6pt); valign default 'top' also anchors the image to the top of its 30pt band (the golden original centered it: measured golden image band [line−28.8, line−8.7] == exactly valign-center).
- Golden-reference study (pdf-template-ref/golden/*.png, the React-PDF Prestige rasters): 'For ...' left-aligned at block edge ✓ (current code already correct), signature image horizontally centered on block center, name/'Authorized Signatory' centered, line spans block. Confirmed via pixel measurement + VLM.
- FIX (both renderers, identical): (A) GT — compute y from font metrics: y = barY + barH/2 − (718/2000)·fontSize for each string (label 8.25 → bar-relative cap band [12.04, 17.96], amount 11.25 → [10.96, 19.04]; both cap-box centers exactly on the 30pt bar's midline, balanced gaps for any amount width/length); documented inline with the metric reasoning. (B) Signature — the 75×30 fit box is now anchored at sigX + (sigBlockW−75)/2 (centered over the block) with valign:'center' added; aspect preserved by fit → correct centering for ANY signature asset dimensions. No other template/layout/business change; wording, colors, typography, totals math, pagination, footer untouched.
- TERMINOLOGY: previous refactor had already migrated the backend package/banner/API.md; found and fixed 4 remaining product-level references: vite.config.ts header comment, src/platform/whatsapp/http.ts doc header, src/components/invoice/InvoiceWhatsAppShare.tsx doc comment, database/old/0002_tables.sql comment ('WhatsApp backend' → 'FUSION ONE backend'). Kept: VITE_WHATSAPP_BACKEND_BASE env var name + its references (compatibility-sensitive identifier), all WhatsApp-subsystem terms (routes /api/whatsapp/*, WhatsAppManager, pairing/states/JID docs in API.md, sender-key Baileys identifiers).
- VALIDATION HARNESS (tool-results/): make_assets.ts (signature PNGs 300×90 / 420×70 wide / 160×160 square / 110×220 tall + logo via @napi-rs/canvas), asset-server.ts (:3199, killed after), gen_pdfs.ts (drives the REAL backend generateInvoicePdf + the frontend buildInvoice template through the same-resolution pdfkit — the browser wrapper's registerStdFonts/Blob path is environment plumbing; AFM metrics identical in both builds), 8 invoice fixtures (normal/short/long amounts, 4 signature aspect ratios, no-sig/no-logo, proforma), verify_pdf.py (pixel measurement of GT bar centering + signature geometry with colored-image detection), rasterized via pdftoppm 150 DPI.
- MEASURED RESULTS — baseline vs fixed: GT label −2.16pt → −0.24…+0.00pt, GT amount −1.68pt → +0.48…+0.84pt (residual = comma-tail ink + antialias; cap/digit mass exactly centered; gaps 11.5/12.0 and 10.6/9.1 balanced); signature image horizontal −33.75pt → −0.48pt, vertical +0.00…−0.24pt (all 4 aspect ratios, both renderers); 'For ...' left-aligned ✓; name/signatory centered ✓ (offsets ≤0.96pt). 112/112 checks PASS (14 page-sets × 8 checks). Multi-page invoice (sale-long, summary on page 2) also verified.
- PARITY: frontend and backend PDFs are content-byte-identical (only pdfkit's per-instance document /ID differs); pdftotext diff baseline→fixed = IDENTICAL for all 16 PDFs (content unchanged).
- CODE QUALITY: frontend tsc --noEmit PASS; backend tsc --noEmit PASS; vitest 138/138 PASS; backend bun --hot picked up pdf.ts cleanly (restart log clean, /health/ready 200, banner fusion-one-backend); vite restarted on vite.config.ts comment change, clean.

- VLM before/after visual comparison (tool-results/cmp-gt-before-after.png, cmp-sig-before-after.png): GT text was sitting high with unbalanced spacing → now properly centered; signature image was clearly shifted left → now centered over the line matching the centered texts.

- WhatsApp subsystem: 9-state runtime machine + 3-value session dimension; single-flight startup (login may pair, wake never pairs); candidate resolution local→Redis with generation-epoch protection; demand-driven retention (SSE presence/startup/sends/reconnect = demand; 5-min disconnect grace WHATSAPP_CLIENT_DISCONNECT_GRACE_MS); QR: Baileys 60s rotation + backend per-second countdown events; reconnect backoff 1s→60s ladder + 0-25% jitter; security codes {401,403,411,440,500} fail-closed (destroySession local+Redis) from authenticated states only; EXPECTED_WHATSAPP_JID identity pin.
- Redis = encrypted session backup ONLY (AES-256-GCM, scrypt key, whitelist of Baileys auth files, 5s connect/10s command timeouts, at-most-one retry on transport failure, debounce 2s/max-wait 10s scheduler).
- Timers: watchdog 10s invariant check; SSE ping 30s/client; QR countdown 1s; grace 5min; reconnect/pairing backoff ladder; send timeout 30s; wake timeout 20s; backup debounce 2s/10s; thumbnail ready 20s + job 10s (unref'd); PDF/worker logo fetch abort 8s; shutdown cancel race 5s. NO cron/queue/scheduler exists.

Stage Summary:
- Payments are 100% RPC-transactional: receive_payment (UPDATE sales.paid/due → INSERT payments_in → INSERT account_transactions credit/payment_in) and pay_purchase (UPDATE purchases.paid/due → INSERT payments_out → INSERT account_transactions debit/payment_out); single plpgsql functions = atomic; SQL in migrations/0003 (mirrored in old/0006). Frontend never writes payment tables directly.
- PaymentDialog (src/components/payments/PaymentDialog.tsx) is the ONLY payment entry point, bound to exactly ONE invoice (sale or purchase); fields = amount (default = due), date (FY-clamped), bank account, payment mode (required for non-cash); no notes/reference field; sale_id/purchase_id is single FK — one payment cannot pay multiple invoices; there is no party-level/unallocated payment flow; PaymentsPage is list-only.
- Sale creation is one atomic create_sale RPC writing financial_years counters + sales + sale_items + inventory_items(sold) + trade-in purchases/inventory/trade_ins + payments_in/account_transactions when paid>0; purchase creation analogous (create_purchase); proforma create_proforma (PI- numbering, no payments).
- Post-create: all three New pages arm sessionStorage 'fusion-one.whatsapp-auto-send' = "{type}:{id}" ONLY if the matching whatsapp_settings auto_send_* flag is on, then navigate to the detail page where InvoiceWhatsAppShare consumes the key on mount, removes it, and POSTs /api/whatsapp/sendInvoice; backend resolves recipient from party.number, caption from whatsapp_settings template (9 vars), renders PDF server-side, sends one document message; failure (e.g. WhatsApp not connected → 503) shows an error toast, key already consumed, no retry.
- Manual delivery exists for sale/purchase/proforma (list ⋮ Share + detail sidebar Send via WhatsApp) and Save PDF/Print exist for the same three types; PDFs are generated client-side (pdfkit) for view/download/print and server-side (backend/src/invoice/pdf.ts) for WhatsApp send. Payments have NO auto-send, NO manual send, NO PDF, NO template, NO recipient resolution — the only payment-adjacent delivery surface is the payment_status template variable in invoice captions.
- Invoice balance: sales.paid/sales.due and purchases.paid/purchases.due are STORED columns maintained by the RPCs; every consumer (sales/purchases lists, detail pages, party ledger, dashboard, PDF builders, WhatsApp caption) reads the stored columns — NOTHING derives due from payments_in/payments_out sums; payments_in/payments_out rows are read only by the Payments listing page.
- Edit invoice = per-item sold_price + date/discount/total/final_total/due via DIRECT chained table writes (update_sale RPC exists but is unused — non-atomic, stale-detail-cache risk); payments are never touched by edit; cancel_sale is one atomic RPC (status=cancelled, restock, per-payment debit reversals dated today, trade-in purchase reversal/cancel, resold list returned); delete_sale is a guarded hard delete (paid=0 + trade-ins in stock); purchases have NO cancel/edit UI; proformas have convert-to-sale (sessionStorage handoff; conversion marks proforma converted non-transactionally after sale creation).

RLS: both new tables SELECT-only for authenticated (can_access_app); writes system-only (service role via backend). RPCs (grant service_role only, revoke anon/authenticated): claim_due_delivery_jobs (FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED), recover_expired_delivery_jobs (lease expiry → retry w/ backoff or fail), complete_delivery_job (success/retry/fail/cancel + reminder chain advance), upsert_reminder_config (config + job reconciliation atomic), trigger_reminder_now.
- Backend: payments/ module (repository + receipt builder + send); invoice/send.ts refactored to accept SupabaseClient (user-context manual OR service-context scheduler — ONE pipeline); receipt renderer extends invoice/pdf.ts (shares prestige primitives); delivery/ module (jobs.ts + scheduler.ts); scheduler wired into app.ts start/shutdown; 4 new routes: POST /api/whatsapp/autoSend (create due-now job, return, scheduler executes after poke), POST /api/whatsapp/sendReceipt (create + execute inline), POST /api/whatsapp/sendReminder (create + execute inline), PUT /api/sales/:id/reminder-settings; SSE += DELIVERY_JOB_RESULT; new error codes PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND/PAYMENT_DIRECTION_INVALID/REMINDER_NOT_ELIGIBLE/DELIVERY_JOB_CONFLICT; config += DELIVERY_POLL_INTERVAL_MS/DELIVERY_JOB_LEASE_MS.
- Reminder semantics: eligibility re-checked at execution (current sales.status + sales.due); first job at config-time + frequency; success increments reminders_sent + creates next job only if still eligible; manual reminder converges on the same executor; at-least-once delivery documented (Baileys cannot guarantee exactly-once).
- Frontend: no second API client (http.ts extended); New*Pages arm auto-send via POST (no sessionStorage); InvoiceWhatsAppShare loses the consumption effect, gains delivery-status line; SaleDetailPage gains Reminders section + dialog; PaymentsPage gains Send Receipt row action; Settings gains 3 template cards (paymentIn/paymentOut/reminder, no autoSend toggles); token vocabulary += payment_amount/payment_date/balance_due; SSE job-result → invalidations + auto-send toasts; NO frontend receipt PDF (receipt is backend-rendered for WhatsApp delivery only, per spec §12/§15/§37 conditionals).

Stage Summary:
- Phase 0 complete: both repos + DB + infra inspected directly; spec internalized; full design settled (see above). DB password is the ONLY external blocker (migrations + DB tests); everything else can proceed. Presentation-level only; all business behavior preserved.

Work Log:
- INSPECTED FIRST: shared Button.tsx (root cause found: `{isLoading && <Loader2 className="mr-2 ...">}` INSERTED the spinner into the flex row → +~24px width during loading), Modal/ConfirmDialog, all 25+ Button isLoading usages across 20 files, every custom spinner implementation (animate-spin grep: PaymentsPage/PaymentsDialog row buttons, InvoiceSidebar SidebarButton, InvoiceWhatsAppShare, WhatsAppPlatformPanel, WhatsAppPairingDialog, DeliveryMonitorPage, InvoicePdfViewer), all conditional-icon patterns (`{!loading && <Icon/>}` ×5 + WhatsAppPairingDialog cancel's spinner-insert + label-swap), and the complete user-facing terminology surface (payments/receipts/statements/reminders/settings/delivery/accounts) incl. tests' accessible-name queries.
- PART A ROOT FIX — shared Button.tsx refactored: children ALWAYS mounted inside a `display: contents` wrapper (boxless — children keep participating in the button's own flex layout, so gap/wrap/margin/icon-sizing behave identically in every state); while loading the wrapper turns `text-transparent` (color INHERITS through display:contents → text + currentColor icons invisible WITHOUT giving up their footprint, and transparent text KEEPS the accessible name for screen readers/tests — critical because paymentsDialog tests query by accessible name DURING states); the spinner lives in an absolutely-positioned overlay (`relative` added to base classes; overlay is a SIBLING of the wrapper so it re-inherits the button's own text color incl. per-usage overrides, contributes NOTHING to intrinsic size); `mr-2` insertion removed. No fixed widths, no measurement, no delays. Chose text-transparent over invisible (visibility:hidden would strip the accessible name) and over opacity (opacity doesn't work through display:contents and an inline-flex wrapper would break the button-level gap/wrap spacing model).
- PART A usage-level fixes (6): WhatsAppPairingDialog Cancel pairing → shared isLoading (removed manual Loader2 insertion + 'Cancelling…' label swap — was resizing mid-action); 5 conditional-icon buttons now render their icons UNCONDITIONALLY (ReminderDialog Send Reminder Now BellRing, PaymentsDialog Send Payment Statement ScrollText, WhatsAppTemplateCard Save, VerifyEmailPage Resend RefreshCw, ProfilePage Send reset link RefreshCw — unmounting would shrink the reserved footprint under the new overlay). - PART B terminology normalization (canonical vocabulary: Receive Payment = record money in; Pay Party = record money out (existing product term); Payment Receipt = document for ONE payment; Send Receipt = send that document; Payment Statement = all-payments document; Payments In/Out = categories; Received/Paid = column labels):
