- CONFIRMED AUDIT FINDINGS: conversion = sessionStorage handoff + non-atomic swallowed proforma status update; trade_ins duplicates device identity; cancel_sale/delete_sale would hit FK violations with trade-ins (delete inventory before trade_ins; untested because 0 rows); EditSalePage does non-atomic direct writes + invalidates non-existent 'parties-page' key; update_sale RPC dead; allocate_bill_numbers dead; no FY-active guard in sale/proforma RPCs; no proforma edit/void; conversion party_id prefill broken (parties embed lacks id); conversion trade-ins prefill imei=''.
- TARGET DESIGN (from spec + repository):
  * trade_ins → (sale_id, inventory_item_id NOT NULL FK, credit_value, mrp, document_url): identity ONLY in inventory_items. Hidden PUR-TRD purchase + purchase_items retained (acquisition trail).
  * inventory_items: identity freeze trigger when status='sold' (brand/model/imei/ram_rom/color immutable; enforced at DB).
  * sales.proforma_id (nullable FK, partial UNIQUE) — the conversion link + hard duplicate-conversion guard.
  * proforma_invoice_items: + inventory_item_id nullable FK; description nullable; CHECK (inventory_item_id IS NOT NULL OR description IS NOT NULL); historical free-text rows stay truthful (no fabricated refs).
  * proforma_trade_ins unchanged (honest PROPOSED model: description/qty/rate/value).
  * ONE canonical create_sale(payload) RPC with proforma-conversion mode: FOR UPDATE lock on proforma (status must be 'active'), party/items/prices/discount from DB (not browser), server-computed totals, trade-in actuals + payment from conversion dialog, link sale→proforma + mark converted in the SAME transaction.
  * cancel_sale: only-active + FY-active guards, FOR UPDATE, FK-safe reversal order (trade_ins → purchase_items → purchases → inventory), resold list with identity via inventory join.
  * delete_sale: only-active + paid=0 + all trade-ins in stock, FK-safe order, reverts originating proforma to 'active' (its sale graph no longer exists).
  * update_sale: canonical atomic edit (server-computed totals, active+FY guards) — EditSalePage switches to it.
  * create_proforma: inventory-referenced items (in-stock, same FY, no dups), server-computed totals, FY-active guard.
  * NEW update_proforma (edit active) + void_proforma (void active, no side effects).
  * create_trade_in_purchase_bill adapted to new trade_ins shape (PUR-26-27 quirk preserved).
  * DROP dead allocate_bill_numbers.
  * Frontend: NewProformaPage becomes stock-picker sibling of sale editor (quoted price per device, no per-line discount for new rows); NEW EditProformaPage; NEW conversion dialog on ProformaDetailPage (actual trade-in details + payment → create_sale proforma mode → navigate to sale); sessionStorage convert_proforma REMOVED; Exchange/SaleDetail/EditSale read identity via inventory join; FE+BE builders map trade-in identity from inventory; invalidation fixed (EditSalePage keys, SalesPage list-cancel inventory).
  * Migrations 0010 (schema) + 0011 (functions) — TEST ONLY.

Stage Summary:
- Environment ambiguity resolved (TEST alive; env restored to TEST; production untouched). Complete current-state understanding from repo + live TEST DB. Target domain model + migration/data strategy designed per spec sections 6-34. Implementation begins with migrations.

  * Identity freeze (sold blocked, prices allowed, in-stock editable).

  * Failure paths: unavailable inventory (clean fail, proforma active, NO partial sale, no counter consumption), invalid trade-in rollback, substitution blocked, paid>final, missing line, legacy user-mapped conversion at quoted value, legacy qty≠1 deterministic rejection.
  * Concurrency: two racing conversions → exactly 1 success/1 failure, exactly ONE sale, exactly ONE counter increment.
  * FY rollover: trade-in device carried forward (provenance preserved), history intact, cancel-in-closed-FY + convert-from-closed-FY rejected.
  * Security: anon EXECUTE revoked, authenticated/service_role granted.
  * Referential integrity sweep: zero dangling rows.

Task ID: RAMROM-UX-1
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: FUSION ONE — Simplify RAM/ROM input behavior: the previous validation-UX implementation silently transformed substantially invalid input ("12GB/256GB" → "12/256"). Replace with accept-or-reject semantics (digits + at most one "/" only; invalid input rejected AS ENTERED, never rewritten). Canonical validator, blur/submit inline message, and everything else in the validation UX unchanged. TEST-only.

Work Log:
- AUDIT: RamRomInput.tsx called onChange(filterRamRomInput(v)); the filter (fields.ts) stripped every non-digit/non-slash char and collapsed extra slashes — silently manufacturing valid values ("12GB/256GB"→"12/256", "12a/256"→"12/256", "12-256"→"12256", "12 / 256"→"12/256"). 5 call sites (Inventory add/edit, NewPurchase, NewSale trade-in, ConvertProformaDialog) all pass value/onChange straight through — fix fully encapsulated in the component + the ONE validation module. DB (migration 0012) enforces ^[0-9]+/[0-9]+$ on new/changed values with legacy rows grandfathered-but-editable — so the entry rule must allow deleting from a legacy value.
- fields.ts: REPLACED filterRamRomInput with acceptsRamRomInput(next, previous): true when next could be produced by allowed typing (/^\d*\/?\d*$/ — "", "12", "12/", "12/256") OR is a pure deletion (shorter + subsequence of previous — guards only what ENTERS the field, keeps legacy DB values editable). Private isSubsequenceOf helper. Module header updated.
- RamRomInput.tsx: onChange now accepts (calls onChange(next)) or rejects (restores the last accepted value to the DOM; onChange NOT called) — never transforms. Doc comment rewritten.
- TESTS: fields.test.ts RAM/ROM input-constraints describe rewritten for the acceptance rule (accepts typing-producible values; rejects 12a/256, 12-256, 12//256, "12 / 256", 12GB/256GB, 12/256GB, abc; "never turns 12GB/256GB into 12/256"; "12/" allowed + judged by validateRamRom on blur; deletion-from-legacy-value allowed, addition-of-junk rejected). NEW src/test/ramRomInput.test.tsx (6 tests) — DOM contract via RTL: per-keystroke accepts, letter keystroke refused, invalid paste refused whole, second "/" + hyphen forms refused, valid paste accepted, deletion from legacy value works.
- RESULTS: bun run test — 13 files / 189 tests PASS (was 12/181; +8 net). bun run typecheck PASS. bun run lint — pre-existing repo-wide ESLint v9 no-config failure only (same as VALUX-1, unrelated). Frontend dev log: zero errors.
- E2E (agent-browser, dev :3000, TEST Supabase owner@fusionone.test): Inventory Add modal — typing "12GB/256GB" per keystroke → "12/256" (letters never enter, same prevention model as IMEI); paste-like whole-value "12GB/256GB" into "12" → REJECTED, field stays "12" (the old code made it "12/256"); pastes "12-256"/"12//256"/"12 / 256"/"12/256GB" all rejected as entered; valid paste "8/128" accepted; "12/" allowed while typing → blur → inline "Enter RAM and storage like 12/256." (role=alert, aria-invalid=true, field-level — no toast) → typing "256" clears error live; full form (Brand/Model/12-256/Color/IMEI/prices) SAVED → row "E2E Test RamRom UX 990000777001234" in table (end-to-end DB path works). Edit modal: DB value 8/128 loads, "GB" typing + "8GB/128GB" paste rejected, cancelled without saving. Zero page/console errors; zero WhatsApp/message-job network calls.
- WHATSAPP SAFETY: no sale/purchase/proforma created (only one inventory item — no WhatsApp flow attaches to inventory); zero autoSend/message requests in the network log; whatsapp_settings flags untouched (frontend-only diff — 4 files, 100% under src/; backend/, database/, migrations untouched).

Stage Summary:
- RAM/ROM entry is now accept-or-reject: only values typing digits and a single "/" can produce ever enter the field; everything else is rejected as entered — the application never silently rewrites user input ("12GB/256GB" can never become "12/256"). Deleting is always allowed (legacy DB values stay editable). The incomplete "12/" is judged by the existing canonical validateRamRom on blur/submit with the existing plain-language inline message.

Task ID: PDOC-FIX-1
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: FUSION ONE — fix the Party Detail → Documents frontend bug: the tab showed "1 active · 0 archived" while the document list/table rendered empty (count populated, row not rendered). Diagnosis-only task constraints: no backend/storage/encryption changes, no new document API, no redesign — trace the response from backend to renderer, fix the actual frontend contract break, add a focused regression test, run the full regression, verify visibly in the UI.

Work Log:
- Traced the complete data flow first (no guessing): PartyDetailPage → PartyDocumentsTab → usePartyDocuments (features/party-documents/api.ts) → GET /api/parties/:partyId/documents → backend routes.ts returns { documents }. Verified the LIVE TEST response (owner login): HTTP 200, { documents: [ { id, party_id, file_name, mime_type, file_size: 302003, checksum_sha256, status: "active", created_at, archived_at } ] } — the shape matches the frontend PartyDocument type field-for-field; one active document for each of Aditya Singh and Rahul Sharma. The counter (derived from the SAME query array via documents.filter) was therefore correct, and DataTable maps rows with no filtering — the document never disappeared from the data, only from the screen.
- Empirically confirmed the root cause with agent-browser geometry BEFORE fixing: the row existed in the DOM (text + 48.5px laid-out height) but the fill-mode table card measured height=2 (border only) and the ListViewport scroller (absolute inset-0) measured height=0 with scrollHeight=85 — the whole table was clipped inside a zero-height scroll region while the toolbar counter above still rendered.
- ROOT CAUSE: PartyDocumentsTab's root was a plain block wrapper (div.space-y-3). ListPage gives its content area a bounded flex column (flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-5); the invoice tabs pass their fill DataTable directly into it, so the card's flex-1/min-h-0 chain resolves. The Documents tab's block wrapper broke that chain: inside a block parent the card's flex-1 is inert (height=content), and its only in-flow child — ListViewport's relative min-h-0 flex-1 wrapper — contains solely absolutely-positioned elements (the inset-0 scroller + fade), so it collapsed to 0 and clipped every row. The bug was invisible to the jsdom suite (no layout engine) and to DOM-text E2E assertions (clipped elements still exist in the DOM) — which is why the refactor session's verification missed it.
- FIX (smallest correct change, in src/components/party-documents/PartyDocumentsTab.tsx only): the tab root becomes a stretching flex item + flex container — "flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-3" (identical 12px rhythm via gap instead of space-y-3) — and the fixed bands (toolbar, error banner, empty state, progress hint) get shrink-0. The fill DataTable card is now a real flex child of a bounded column, so its rows viewport resolves to actual height. No backend, API, query, data, or visual-design change; the existing Party Documents infrastructure (routes, R2, AES-256-GCM, queries, actions, in-app preview dialog) was reused untouched.
- Focused regression test added (src/test/partyDocumentsTab.test.tsx): "keeps the list-page height chain so counted rows are actually rendered" — renders the tab inside ListPage's bounded flex column exactly as PartyDetailPage does, asserts the counter and the SAME document's row, then pins the structural height-chain contract (root carries flex/min-h-0/flex-1/flex-col and never space-y-3; the fill card carries flex/min-h-0/flex-1; the absolute inset-0 rows viewport contains the row). This is the guard that fails on exactly this bug's reintroduction.
- Full verification: FE vitest 264/264 (263 prior + 1 new); FE tsc clean; BE tsc clean; BE bun test 48/48; DB suites party-documents 24/24 + tradein-proforma 121/121 + validation-search 135/135. dev.log clean (only the PartyDocumentsTab HMR update). agent-browser E2E on TEST (owner login): both parties' Documents tabs render the real row (ChatGPT Image … .png | JPEG | 295 KB | 07 Oct 2026 | Active | View) with "1 active · 0 archived" matching the one visible row; active row menu = Download/Replace/Archive; View opens the IN-APP preview dialog (image at natural 1672×941, Close + separate Download, exactly ONE browser tab, no window.open); row Download fired GET …/documents/:id/download 200; empty-state party shows "No documents yet." + the dashed empty state with no table; mobile (390×844) renders the row in the card layout inside a real-height scroller. Screenshots: download/documents-tab-fixed.png + download/documents-tab-final-verification.png.
- TEST state re-verified after everything: parties 4, party_documents 2, message_jobs 0; the newest sale/purchase/trade-in rows (SAL-2027-28-0004, PUR-TRD-2027-28-0003, +1 trade_in, all 2026-10-07T14:07Z) are the owner's own pre-session activity — identical before and after my suite runs; zero data drift introduced.

Stage Summary:
- Root cause was a pure frontend layout-contract break (block wrapper collapsing the list-page flex height chain → zero-height clipped table), NOT a backend/storage/encryption/API problem — the backend response shape and the document record were always correct.
- One file changed for the fix (PartyDocumentsTab.tsx root + fixed bands), one test file extended (+1 regression test); backend, database, R2, encryption, routes and all document actions reused unchanged; Replace still exists only on Party Detail → Documents; preview still in-app only.
- Quality gates: FE 264/264 + tsc; BE 48/48 + tsc; DB 24+121+135; browser-verified visible row on desktop + mobile with counts matching; TEST baseline preserved; WhatsApp safety (message_jobs = 0).

Task ID: PDOC-FIX-2
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: FUSION ONE — the user reported the backend not starting/restarting along with the frontend: every /backend/api/* request from the preview URL returned HTTP 500 (status, events SSE, party documents). Diagnose why the backend was down and restore it durably without duplicating instances.

Work Log:
- Confirmed the symptom's mechanism first: port 3001 had NO listener while the frontend (:3000) was healthy — the 500s are the Vite dev-server proxy failing to connect to its dead target (ECONNREFUSED per dev.log), not backend errors.
- Reconstructed the cause from .zscripts/dev.log + dev.log: at 20:11 the platform re-ran the sandbox dev boot and TWO concurrent .zscripts/dev.sh instances raced. Run #2's stop_stale_servers() (pid-file kill + pkill -f 'bun --hot src/index.ts') SIGTERM'd run #1's freshly-started backend two seconds after it listened; run #2 itself was killed mid-boot before starting its own backend, leaving exactly one survivor tree (frontend vite pid 974) and no backend.
- Discovered the sandbox's process-reaping constraint empirically: backends I started directly from tool invocations (plain &; then setsid+nohup detached session leaders) were killed within ~45s — even a plain `sleep 300` died — while platform-booted processes (same cgroup, env) survive indefinitely. Conclusion: processes must be launched from the platform's own process tree to persist; my tool-session spawns are reaped regardless of detach.
- Solution (also the user's literal ask — "backend starting/restarting along with the frontend"): vite.config.ts gained a dev-only companion plugin, ensureBackendCompanion(). On every Vite dev-server (re)start it probes 127.0.0.1:3001; ONLY if nothing is listening it spawns `bun run dev` in backend/ (PORT=3001 HOST=0.0.0.0, stdio appended to dev.log) as a detached child of the Vite process — i.e., inside the platform-owned process tree with inherited environment, exactly like the dev.sh boot path. Guarded: when dev.sh already started the backend the probe succeeds and the plugin does nothing, so the normal boot order (backend first, then vite) is unchanged; no double-spawn on vite self-restarts (verified: touched config → vite restarted → still exactly ONE backend process, pid unchanged).
- Verification: backend came up instantly via the plugin (pid 1658, /health/live 200); survived the reaper window and stayed alive 4.5+ minutes (vs ~45s for direct spawns); full browser E2E re-run — login, Documents tab — and the exact three endpoints from the user's console all now return HTTP 200 through the preview path: /backend/api/status, /backend/api/events (SSE reconnected), /backend/api/parties/9aa44a17-.../documents; the Documents tab still renders the real row (counter "1 active · 0 archived" + visible row, previous layout fix intact). Screenshot: download/backend-restored-documents-tab.png.
- Regression with the new config: FE vitest 264/264 + tsc clean (the plugin's configureServer never runs in tests; imports are node-safe).
- No application/backend code changes: backend/src untouched, no new API, no data changes; TEST DB untouched (party_documents 2, message_jobs 0 — backend restart is stateless; WhatsApp stays unpaired/idle, WHATSAPP_NOT_CONNECTED on wake as always).

Stage Summary:
- Root cause of the outage: a 20:11 platform dev-boot race (duplicate dev.sh runs killing each other's "stale" backend) + the sandbox reaper that kills any process not launched from the platform's own tree (why a manual restart could not persist).
- Durable fix: the Vite dev server now self-heals the backend — on (re)start it starts the backend if :3001 is silent, as a platform-tree child; normal dev.sh boots are unaffected (probe-guarded). A full sandbox restart also boots both cleanly as always.
- Verified: all previously-500 endpoints return 200 through the preview path; backend stable 4.5+ min; FE 264/264 + tsc; single instance, no double-spawn; Documents tab fully functional again.

- ARCHITECTURE (spec §5/§19): rewrote src/features/reports as ONE coherent pipeline: catalogue.ts (16 declarative report definitions) → data.ts (per-report normalized datasets assembled from the SAME analytics fold + metrics — no new business calculations) → excel/renderer.ts (ONE generic worksheet engine driven by table/summary blocks; no per-report rendering code) → excel/workbook.ts (individual exports + the consolidated pack through the SAME renderer) → export.ts (store-branded filenames + browser download). Deleted excel/charts.ts + excel/sheets.ts (the old 9-sheet chart workbook). ExcelJS stays the only spreadsheet engine (dynamic import).
- REPORT CATALOGUE (spec §8): all 16 reports implemented — Sales Register, Sales Summary, Item-wise Sales, Purchase Register, Purchase Summary, Payments Register, Customer Outstanding, Receivables Ageing, Supplier Payables, Party Statement, Inventory Register, Inventory Valuation, Inventory Ageing, Trade-In Register, Proforma Register, Day Book (an honest chronological voucher register — documented as NOT an accounting journal). Period-filtered reports use the correct business date per domain (invoice/purchase/payment/proforma dates; inventory ageing uses the origin-chain acquisition date); outstanding/inventory reports are current-position reports with explanatory notes.
- ITEM NAME (spec §12): Sales Register columns are exactly Date, Invoice No., Item Name, Customer, Total Amount, Received, Balance, Status — Item Name immediately after Invoice No., joined readably for multi-item invoices ("Samsung Galaxy S24, Nothing Phone 3"), honest '—' fallback, invoice totals never duplicated (one row per invoice). Same column set in the pack's Sales Register sheet and the web Analytics > Sales register table (web register gained the Item Name column).
- STORE BRANDING (spec §6): the configured store (name, address, phone from the singleton store row via the shared useStore query) is the primary heading on every sheet; FUSION ONE is never the business heading; workbook file metadata carries the store name; filenames are {StoreSlug}-{Report}-FY-2026-27.xlsx (fallback without store prefix when unconfigured). Missing-store handled cleanly (title-first heading, no fictional business).
- NO QUANTITY (spec §7): zero quantity/units columns anywhere (the only schema quantity fields — proforma quotation line qty — are never surfaced); counts are labeled as Invoices/Bills/Records document counts.
- EXCEL DESIGN (spec §13-17): cell-based worksheets only — no floating shapes, no charts, no dashboard mimicry. Standard layout: store masthead → contact line → report title → Period/FY/Generated meta → blocks. Real numeric ₹ cells, real date cells, right-aligned money, hairline row borders, restrained slate+single-accent theme, autoFilter + frozen panes on every register, fit-to-width A4 print setup with repeated header rows and page footers, honest empty states ("No records in this reporting period."), exclusion/semantics notes.
- UI (spec §8/§9/§20): AnalyticsReportsPage rewritten inside the existing Analytics > Reports section (no new tab/sidebar): Complete Report Pack primary action + the catalogue grouped into the 5 categories, each card with title/description/export, per-report filters (payments direction; party selector for Party Statement), loading/error/empty-period states, toasts. Existing design system components reused.
- TEST DATA (spec §23): database/tools/seed-reports-fixtures.ts — deterministic UUIDs, idempotent, --cleanup restores baseline. FY 2026-27 (closed, direct SQL matching every invariant incl. the inventory identity-validation trigger): 3 fixture parties, 2 real purchases (Apr/Aug, one partial), 7 sales May-Sep 2026 incl. the multi-item invoice + 2 trade-ins with hidden PUR-TRD bills, payments across UPI/Card/Cash and months, a draft proforma, outstanding sales in all four ageing buckets, stock aged 0-30/31-60/90+. FY 2027-28 (active): the recovery scenario executed through the REAL cancel_sale + create_trade_in_purchase_bill RPCs (resold trade-in → cancelled hidden bill → PUR-2027-28-0006 recovery bill). FY counters maintained; unrelated TEST data untouched.
- NUMERICAL VERIFICATION (spec §24/§27): database/tools/verify-reports-reconciliation.ts (read-only, TEST-only safety abort) — INDEPENDENT SQL truth == buildReportDatasets == Excel cell values for BOTH financial years: 78/78 checks (FY 2026-27: 13 invoices/₹360,493/₹296,998/₹63,495; purchases 4/₹436,500/₹50,000; money in/out ₹321,998/₹386,500; stock 8 records/₹143,000/₹170,494; trade-ins 4/₹21,000; proformas 3; ageing buckets 24,998/8,499/10,999/18,999; FY 2027-28 incl. the cancelled sale + recovery bill exclusions) + a September-only period window (FY boundary filtering). Writes the REAL workbooks to download/.
- AUTOMATED TESTS (spec §28): reportsData.test.ts (28 tests — controlled scenario with hand-computed expected values: item names/multi-item join, cancelled + virtual exclusions incl. both virtual rules, ageing buckets, position reports, party statement, day book, period filtering, empty period) + reportsExcel.test.ts (20 tests — sheet names, store masthead (never FUSION ONE), Item Name after Invoice No., no quantity headers, numeric ₹ cells, real dates, autoFilter + frozen panes + print titles via raw OOXML inspection, totals reconciliation, empty-state workbook, 16-sheet pack + Report Index contents, zero chart/drawing parts) + analyticsPages.test.tsx report flows updated (pack export, individual export, full catalogue rendering, filters). Full suite 422/422; root + backend tsc clean.
- VISUAL VERIFICATION (spec §26 — mandatory gate): LibreOffice-rendered the REAL workbooks to PDF/PNG and VLM-inspected every representative sheet (Sales/Purchase/Payments Registers, Customer Outstanding, Receivables Ageing, Inventory Register/Valuation, Trade-In, Proforma, Report Index, pack sheets). Found 2 defects: (1) long joined item names clipped in the Purchase Register; (2) the meta line truncated on narrow summary sheets. Fixed both (text cells wrap + auto row heights; masthead/note lines unmerged so text overflows into empty cells), regenerated, re-rendered and re-inspected — both confirmed resolved, no remaining defects; the corrected pack re-inspected (17 pages).
- BROWSER E2E (live TEST, agent-browser): logged in (owner.e2e@fusionone.test), verified the Reports catalogue renders all 16 reports + filters in both FYs, exported from the BROWSER and re-parsed the downloads: Sales Register (13 rows, ₹360,493/₹296,998/₹63,495 — exactly the SQL truth; store-branded filename), Complete Report Pack (16 sheets), Party Statement for the selected party (Anaya Gupta, chronological transactions, joined multi-item details), Payments Register with Incoming filter (15 In rows + filter note), Supplier Payables. Web Analytics > Sales register shows the Item Name column incl. the joined multi-item value. Mobile 390px: no horizontal overflow, VLM-verified clean. ZERO console/page errors across the session.
- REGRESSION: backend bun test 48/48 + backend tsc clean (backend untouched); DB suites tradein-proforma 121/121 + validation-search 135/135 (self-cleaning; reports fixtures unaffected — re-verified 78/78 afterwards); frontend vitest 422/422; root tsc clean. `bun run lint` still fails repo-wide with the PRE-EXISTING missing-eslint-config condition (documented before this task; tsc + vitest are the working gates).
- CLEANUP (spec §31): removed excel/charts.ts + excel/sheets.ts, the obsolete old-architecture workbook artifact, the scratch inspection scripts; grep-verified zero references to the old export API and zero TODO/FIXME/placeholder strings in the new modules. Restored the dev backend (:3001) that was down at session start.

Stage Summary:
- The Reports system is fully reimplemented per the specification: one shared architecture (definitions → datasets → generic renderer), 16 individual Excel exports as the primary experience, the consolidated Complete Report Pack with a Report Index, real store branding, Item Name immediately after Invoice No., no quantity fields, professional cell-based worksheets with Excel-native filters/freeze/print, controlled TEST data incl. the recovery lifecycle, and the complete verification chain (SQL truth == report data == Excel cells == browser downloads; structural + visual + E2E + regression all green). - Dumped the LIVE RPC bodies from TEST Supabase: create_sale/create_purchase ALREADY enforce date-within-FY + active-FY; create_trade_in_purchase_bill does NOT (hardcodes current_date with the sale's FY); cancel_sale stamps reversal ledger rows with today + the sale's FY.
- TEST DB audit (read-only): FY 2026-27 closed, FY 2027-28 active (intentional state, set by explicit user-driven close_financial_year; no auto-close triggers); store active FY = 2027-28. FY 2027-28 purchases: PUR-TRD-0003 (active, 2027-04-01), PUR-2027-28-0004 (real, 2027-04-02, 7500/4000/3500), PUR-TRD-0005 (cancelled, 2027-04-05), PUR-2027-28-0006 (recovery, active, date 2026-10-09 — MISDATED). Full-table date/FY audit: exactly TWO mismatched rows in the whole DB — the recovery bill + its sibling sale_cancelled reversal ledger row (both created by seed-reports-fixtures.ts runRecoveryScenario while the sandbox clock was outside the sale's FY). Zero mismatches in sales/payments/proformas.
- Analytics recomputation for FY 2027-28 (independent SQL): REAL bills=1 value=7500 paid=4000 outstanding=3500 — the screenshot's summary is CORRECT per the agreed business rules; the visible 4-record list is the Purchases MANAGEMENT list (FY-scoped, unclassified). The genuine defects: (a) the misdated recovery bill (RPC + seed), (b) virtualBillCount filters status='active' so the cancelled in-period PUR-TRD-0005 is not counted (footer says 1, should count 2), (c) footer wording "1 internal acquisition records" (grammar).
- Excel inspection: unzipped every worksheet of all six download/ workbooks — registers carry ONE conventional frozen pane (ySplit=6, topLeftCell=A7, activePane=bottomLeft, state=frozen); LibreOffice Calc interactive screenshots (Xvfb+ffmpeg) + print PDF renders + VLM inspection: NO duplicated content/panes. The ONE structural defect: the renderer's two-phase view assignment leaves a DEGENERATE zero-extent frozen pane (<pane topLeftCell="A1" activePane="bottomLeft" state="frozen"/>) on the table-less Business Summary sheet — an unconventional view declaration (a "frozen" pane with no frozen rows/columns) that stricter viewers can render as a second/split view.

Stage Summary:
- Root causes established: (1) one-bill summary is correct per business rules — the mismatch is the misdated recovery bill + under-counted exclusion footer + ungrammatical wording; (2) PUR-2027-28-0006's date came from create_trade_in_purchase_bill's current_date (the only purchase RPC without date-in-FY validation) executed by the seed with the clock outside the sale's FY — FY configuration itself is intentional, not defective; (3) the Excel two-view defect's demonstrable structural root is the degenerate zero-split frozen pane from the two-phase view assignment in the shared renderer (register views are already conventional).

Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: Correct FUSION ONE's Excel export presentation defects permanently (duplicated worksheet view, AutoFilter arrows, gridlines/professional polish) and the FY 2027-28 Sales Register "31 March 2027 invoice" financial-year filtering defect — root-caused, fixed, regression-tested, and verified against real generated files and the live TEST app.

Work Log:
- INVESTIGATION (before any change): read the shared renderer (src/features/reports/excel/renderer.ts), theme, data builders, workbook assembly, export path; unzipped the CURRENT generated workbooks from download/ and read their raw worksheet XML — every register sheet carried `<pane ySplit="6" topLeftCell="A7" activePane="bottomLeft" state="frozen"/>` + `<selection pane="bottomLeft" .../>` AND `<autoFilter ref="A6:H21"/>`; read ExcelJS 4.4.0's actual source (sheet-view-xform.js, cell-xform.js, utils.js): a `{showGridLines:false}` view emits exactly `<sheetView showGridLines="0" workbookViewId="0"/>` (no pane, no selection), and `dateToExcel()` serializes a Date from RAW UTC MILLISECONDS with NO rounding.
- ROOT CAUSE 1 (duplicated view): the frozen-pane view on every register sheet — a pane declaration some viewers render as a split/repeated view of rows 1-6. Per the user's explicit instruction the shared generator now configures ONE normal view with NO pane at all: `ws.views = [{ showGridLines: false }]` on EVERY sheet (registers, item sheets, Business Summary alike) → single `<sheetView showGridLines="0" workbookViewId="0"/>`.
- ROOT CAUSE 2 (AutoFilter arrows): `ws.autoFilter = {from, to}` on the primary register — REMOVED from the shared renderer (no worksheet autoFilter, no Excel tables, no filter chrome anywhere).
- ROOT CAUSE 3 (FY filtering / "31 Mar 2027 invoice in the FY 2027-28 register"): DB inspection (tools/inspect-fy-sales.ts, read-only) proved the TEST data CLEAN — every sale date inside its stored FY; the FY 2027-28 register legitimately contains SAL-2027-28-0004 dated **2027-04-01**. The defect was DATE SERIALIZATION: data.ts `excelDate()` built LOCAL-midnight Dates while ExcelJS serializes UTC ms — in IST (UTC+5:30) `new Date(2027,3,1)` = 2027-03-31T18:30Z → serial 46477.7708… → Excel renders **31 Mar 2027**. Fixed: `new Date(Date.UTC(y, m-1, d))` (exact integer day serial in every timezone); `dateKey()` switched to UTC getters so register sort order is timezone-exact too. NO data rewritten, NO records hidden — the historical transactions were already correct.
- PRESENTATION (professional accounting-register look): background gridlines hidden (the single normal view), restrained header band + hairline row separators + right-aligned ₹ figures retained from the theme (theme.ts doc canon updated); masthead/store/title/period/FY/timestamp, column structure and totals all preserved; print setup preserved (A4 fit-to-width, `_xlnm.Print_Titles` repeating rows 1-6 on printed pages, `Page &P of &N` footer); no charts/drawings/decorations anywhere.

The tree now contains exactly ONE renderer.
- REGRESSION TESTS: reportsExcel.test.ts rewritten to the new contract — every sheet of every workbook: EXACTLY ONE `<sheetView>` (with `showGridLines="0"`), ZERO `<pane>`, no frozen/split state, no pane-scoped selection, NO `<autoFilter>`/`<tableParts>`/`xl/tables/`; totals row reconciles; NEW timezone-exact date-serial tests read the RAW worksheet XML and assert the serial equals `25569 + Date.UTC(...)/86400000` exactly (integer, no fractional part) for the 1 Apr 2026 and 31 Mar 2027 boundary sales; print titles/footer/no-charts retained; deduplicated rows/shared-strings retained. reportsData.test.ts gained the FY 2027-28 register-level boundary test (1 Apr 2027 invoice INCLUDED and rendering on its exact UTC day; 31 Mar 2027 invoice of the prior year NEVER included).

- BROWSER E2E (live TEST app, browser relaunched under **Asia/Calcutta**): logged in (owner.e2e@fusionone.test) → Analytics → Sales (FY 2027-28 default) → Export dialog (Financial Year preset, 4/1/2027 → 3/31/2028) → Export Excel — captured the browser-generated blob via a createObjectURL hook and inspected its raw XML: single normal view `showGridLines="0"`, ZERO panes, ZERO autoFilter, date serial **46478 = 2027-04-01** (not 46477.77 = 31 Mar 2027 — the exact user scenario, fixed); same for the browser-generated Purchase Register (serial 46479 = 2027-04-02); ZERO console/page errors; both browser files archived in download/ (*_BROWSER-IST.xlsx).

Task ID: SB-1 (FUSION ONE — stale sandbox restoration: root-cause investigation + permanent fix)
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: Make sandbox startup and recovery consistently restore the correct, current FUSION ONE project without resurrecting stale code, deleted files, or legacy project contents; fix the root cause, not just the symptoms; verify across restoration cycles.

Work Log:
- RESTORATION CHAIN TRACED (before any change): /start.sh wipes /home/z/my-project (preserving the upload mount) and extracts /home/sync/repo.tar whenever that archive exists; repo.tar is written by the platform's pre-stop from the git-committed state (proven: the 07:28 archive contains EXACTLY the 471 files of commit 7eb5ea8 plus .git — no working-tree-only content). A second, platform-controlled mechanism then runs OUTSIDE start.sh: the Z.ai control service materializes a per-conversation file store into /tmp/my-project and clones every store entry that is not tracked in git back into the working tree. /tmp/my-project/.initial_snapshot.json (787 entries) + .pending_clone.json (355 files = store minus git-tracked set, exactly) are its manifests.
- ROOT CAUSE (evidence-based): at this container's boot the tar restore completed at 16:07:48 and the platform clone recreated 295 files at 16:08:03–16:08:16 with PRESERVED October-7 mtimes — FUSIONONE-MASTER/ (293 files, the pre-promotion project duplicate git-removed by fae9f6b), TradeInDocumentField.tsx + tradeInDocumentField.test.tsx (both removed by 9bacfc9's party-documents architecture). The platform auto-commit at 16:50:06 (59c2ffe, git add -A) then re-tracked all of it into git. repo.tar itself was verified CLEAN — the stale content came exclusively from the conversation file store, which never recorded the pre-genesis deletions. The store prunes entries absent from both tree and git (proven: sheets.ts/charts.ts, deleted by 07210a2, were in the 16:08 staging materialization but absent from the 16:50 snapshot), so files that were resurrected into the tree before a prune survived — the clone-before-prune race.
- F1-3 WORK PRESERVED: the current renderer.ts single-view fix, data.ts UTC dates, theme, tests and worklog were verified intact before cleanup (they were live edits in this container at 16:31–16:49, committed by 59c2ffe); F1-2's uncommitted verification tools recovered by the store (verify-analytics-reconciliation.ts, verify-reports-reconciliation.ts) and the recovered download/ artifacts were deliberately KEPT (recovered legitimate work, not stale code).
- CLEANUP (commit 310e0f6): git rm of FUSIONONE-MASTER/ (293 files, zero live references — dev.sh runs the root app + backend/, tsconfig/vitest anchored at ./src), TradeInDocumentField.tsx + its test (zero imports outside the pair; both deleted by 9bacfc9), plus the two untracked stale env-file leftovers (rm). Making the files absent from both tree and git is what lets the store prune them at its next sync.
- GUARD (commit 4846b71): .zscripts/verify-canonical-restore.sh — required-layout check, canonical revision pin (.zscripts/canonical-rev = 310e0f6, ancestor-of-HEAD check that detects a stale repo.tar restoration), known-stale blacklist (each entry carries its recorded deletion commit), and nested-project-duplicate detection (reports unknowns, never deletes them). dev.sh runs it early (--pre-restore) and as a pgrep-guarded periodic background guard (first check 120s after boot, then every 300s — the clone was observed ~25s after boot AND at a session event 42 min later). Failures log loudly; confirmed-stale paths are auto-removed before the platform auto-commit can re-track them. Detection tested by simulating a resurrection: the guard removed the fake FUSIONONE-MASTER + TradeInDocumentField and failed with exit 1 as designed.
- repo.tar REGENERATED from the canonical state (git-tracked files + .git, the platform's own format) and verified by FOUR full extraction cycles into temp dirs: HEAD = 4846b71, working tree clean, 494 tracked files, all 494 hash-identical to the live tree, zero stale entries, F1-3 fixes present in the restored copy. The live /home/sync/repo.tar now carries the canonical state (25,292,800 bytes, 1,819 entries) so even a failed pre-stop restores the correct project.
- GATES: FE vitest 465/465 (32 files — exactly the previous 472 minus the removed stale test file's 7); FE tsc --noEmit clean; BE 48/48; BE tsc clean; bun run lint remains the pre-existing ESLint-10-without-flat-config gap documented by F1-3 (unrelated to this task).
- BROWSER E2E (live TEST app from the cleaned tree): login owner.e2e@fusionone.test → dashboard renders with full navigation (FY 2027–2028) → Analytics > Purchase tab renders live KPI data (bills/paid/owed, Apr 2027 trend 7,500) — ZERO page errors, ZERO console errors/warnings; screenshot archived (download/sandbox-restore-verification.png).

Stage Summary:

Fixed by removing the stale set from both tree and git (prune condition), regenerating repo.tar from the canonical state, and adding a loud, tested restoration guard to the dev lifecycle.

- Not re-testable from inside the sandbox: an actual container restart / a fresh platform sync run (the store manifests have not been rewritten since 16:50:05); the guard + regenerated tar are the defense layers for those cycles.
